patippurai
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimaiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolainōkkup pārvaiyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum cērttu avaruṭaiya maṟaivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭuraikaḷai ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉaivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭuraip putaiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷai naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉaivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟaiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamaiyuṭaivar eṉṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭaikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟaiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñarai iṉṉum peṟṟut taravillai eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturaiyarum, kuṟaimatiyar tēkkivaitta karaiyiruḷai nīkka vanta maṟaimalaiyār vaḻivanta niṟaimalaiyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravaittalaivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉaivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumaikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉaiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam uraippaṉa. tamiḻai āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimaippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyai mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟai ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamaiyākum.
pāvāṇarait tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkaikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilaittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talaippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟaikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟaiyum, citaikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyaiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putaiyalkaḷai tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyai mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamaiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷai ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tantai periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷkai
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkaikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷaip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

teṉcoṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷ

1
vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mup perum pirivukaḷā yaṭaṅkumeṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟiṉām. avaṟṟuḷ  āriyak kavaiyil Teutonic kiḷaikkut talaimaiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutam; ēṉaiya German, Dutch, English mutaliyaṉa. 
mēṉāṭṭu moḻiyārāyccip pulavar, ilakkaṇa oṟṟumaiyālum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷiṉ oṟṟumaiyālum vaṭamoḻiyaic cerumaṉ moḻikku iṉamākak kaṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, intiya āriyarum ceru kṉiyarumxruflattilxrētkuppiṉarāyxrēyiṭattil(mēlāciyā)tḻntirukkanvaṇṭum.rÇ¤âu nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kiṟittu vukkup  piṟpaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, avarkku muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷai yellām uṇartaṟku moḻinūlē ciṟanta karuviyākiṉṟatu. oru vakuppārai iṉṉā reṉṟu uṇartaṟku avaratu pēcciṉum ciṟanta karuvi piṟitoṉṟillai.
nilattiṟ kiṭantamai kālkāṭṭuṅ kāṭṭuṅ
kulattiṟ piṟantārvāyc col.	(kuṟaḷ. 959)
ākavē, intiya āriyar cerumāṉiyarkku iṉameṉṟum avar pēciya moḻiyum cerumaṉukku iṉameṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. atai avviru moḻikaṭkum iṉṟiyamaiyāta cila potuc coṟkaḷāṟ kāṭṭutum. iṅkilīṣceruma‹moḻiyiṉiṉṟu«piṟantatātaliṉ,kīḻvaru«iṅkilīZcoṟkaḷellā«cerumaṉeṉwteḷika. (Jutes, Saxons, Angles eṉṟa mūvakaic cerumāṉiya vakuppiṉarē ki.mu. 449-l cerumaṉiyiṉiṉṟum pirittāṉiyāvukkup pōy iṅkilīṣ moḻiyai vaḻaṅkiṉavar.)
English	camaṟkirutam	English 	camaṟkirutam
iṉi ilakkaṇa oṟṟumaikaḷāvaṉa: 
1. mey moḻi mutalātal
2. orumai irumai paṉmai eṉa eṇ mūṉṟātal
3. peyareccaṅkaḷ pāl eṇ vēṟṟumaikaḷaik kāṭṭal
4. ilakkaṇap pāl (grammatical gender)
5. viṉaimuṟṟukaḷ pāl kāṭṭāmai
6. payaṉilai viṉaiccollāyē yiruttal 
ivai pōṉṟavai iṉṉum ettuṇaiyō uḷa. mukkiyamāṉavai maṭṭum iṅku ctāraṇamākakFṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
MriyamvṉṉumFṟiyīṭuKjṉmutalāÇyakḻikaḷešlāva‰ṟiṟkumgtuvāyvaHṅkiṟṟu.pi‹ò Teutonic vakuppukku maṭṭum vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉpu intiya āriya moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutattiṟkē ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. intiya āriyar nāl vakuppārāyiṉum, avarkaḷukkuḷ pirāmaṇar maṭṭum ik kuṟiyīṭṭait tamakkum tam moḻikkum varaiyaṟuttukkoṇṭaṉar.
āriya vakuppu moḻikaḷuḷ Latin, Greek eṉṟa iraṇṭum muṟpaṭṭavai. Teutonic moḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavai. airōppiya aracukaṭkellām muntiya kirēkka carittiram ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭi ṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ākavē, camaṟkiruta  āriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉatum atē kālamāyttā ṉirukkavēṇṭum. 
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻ. tamiḻar (ki.mu. 8000) kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟum vaṭakkē pōyc ciṉṉa āciyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyak kukai veṭṭālum kuḻi veṭṭālum teriya varukiṉṟatu. aṅkētāṉ tamiḻ cēmiyak (Semitic) kiḷaiyākat tirintatu. epirēyam (Hebrew), arapi (Arabic) mutaliyaṉa cēmiyak kiḷai moḻikaḷ. avaṟṟaiyaṭuttu Latin, Greek mutaliya āriyak kiḷai moḻikaḷ piṟantaṉa. oru tāymoḻiyiṉiṉṟum oru kiḷaimoḻi uṇṭāyiruppiṉ ataṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta mutaṟ coṟkaḷellām tāymoḻiyā yirukkum. ēṉaiyavellām ataṟkuc contamāṉa ākkac coṟkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷ oru pakuti piṟamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉkoṇṭa ticaiccoṟkaḷā yirukkum. oru moḻiyiṉ ākkaccoṟ perukkattiṟkum curukkattiṟkum kāraṇam am moḻiyai vaḻaṅkum makkaḷaṟivē yākum. 
āriyak kiḷaikaḷil oṉṟākiya Teutonic eṉpatu Greko-Latin eṉṟa peruṅkiḷaiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷaittapoḻutē (atāvatu camaṟkiruta   āriyar intiyāvukku varumuṉṉamē) atil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa  tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantiruntaṉa. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvukku vantu tamiḻa rōṭu (ki.mu. 2500) kalantapiṉ eṇṇiṟanta teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantaṉa. āriyar varumuṉṉum vantapoḻutum vaṭaintiyāvil vaḻaṅkivantavai yellām koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷē. 
āriya varukaikku muṉ camaṟkirutattil allatu Teutonic moḻikaḷiṟ kalantirunta teṉcoṟkaḷiṟ cila:

 				vesture		vastram
camaṟkirutattiṟku mutaṟkāviyam irāmāyaṇam. irāmar iruntatu iṭaiccaṅka kālam. ākavē, tolkāppiyamum akattiyamum irāmāyaṇattiṟku muṟpaṭṭaṉavām. vaṭamoḻikku mutal ilakkaṇam pāṇiṉīyam. pāṇiṉi pāratattiṟkum piṟpaṭṭavar. pāratakālam 1000 B.C.; irāmāyaṇa kālamō 2000 B.C.; ākavē, pāṇiṉīyam tolkāppiyat tiṟkuk kuṟaintatu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. āriya varukaikku muṉṉamē tamiḻar muttamiḻilum palkalaikaḷilum tērntirun taṉar. akattiyam vaḻinūlē yaṉṟi mutaṉūlākātu. itaṉ virivai yellām ematu mutaṟṟāymoḻiyiṟ (The Classica Prima) kaṇṭukoḷka. 
iṉi, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷāvaṉa: 
matam 
	mati + am = matam.
tēy + u = tēyvu - tēvu - tē - tī.
tēyvu + am - tēyvam - teyvam Gk. Theos.
tēyvam - tēvam - tēvaṉ. 
teyvam eṉpatu mutalāvatu neruppaiyum piṉpu civattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. (tiruvācaka virivurai kāṇka.) 
civa + m = civam - civaṉ - civai.
uru + tiram = uruttiram - uruttiraṉ - rutraṉ = ciṉaṉ. 
uruttal = ciṉattal, tiram toḻiṟpeyar vikuti. 
ari + am = aram - araṉ.
ō - ōm - ōṅkāram or ōkāram. 
ōveṉpatu mūla volikkuṟippu. ol, oli, ōlam, ōcai, ōtai mutaliya ōcaipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām okara ōkāra mutaṉmoḻikaḷātal kāṇka. rī eṉpatu rīṅkāramāṉāṟ pōla ō eṉpatu ōṅkāramāyiṟṟu. 
uru + tiram + ākkam = uruttirākkam - ruttirākṣam (uruttirākka viḷakkaṅ kāṇka.) 
vi + pūti = vipūti. puḻuti - pūti; vi, upacarkkam.
āṇavam: āḷ - āṇ - āṇmai - āṇavam.
māy + ai = māyai: ai. - to. pe.vi.
kāmi + am = kāmiyam, kām + iyam  eṉṟum pirikkalām.
iyam oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
kām + am = kāmam. 
kām + ar = kāmar. 
kumaraṉ, kumari - kommai(tiraṭci) eṉṉum paṇpiṉaṭiyākap  piṟantavai. cf. virgin Gk. orago - to swell. cēy (Zeus), murukaṉ, vēlaṉ eṉpaṉa kumaraṟku maṟupeyarkaḷ. 
caṇṭikai - caḷ - caḷḷai - caṇṭai - caṇṭaṉ - caṇṭi -caṇṭikai. 
kāḷi : kaḷ - kaḷḷam - kāḷam - kāḷi.
             - kaḷam - kaḷaṅkam = karuppu. 
ciṉaṉ : ciṉa + am = ciṉam - ciṉaṉ.
viṣṇu : viṇ - viṇṭu. viṇṇaippōl nīlamāyum eṅkum   niṟaintumiruppavaṉ. 
māyōṉ : mā - māy - māyōṉ.  mā = karuppu.
kiruṣṇaṉ: kaḷ - kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ. 
māyōṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ kiruṣṇāvatārattiṟ putitāy uṇṭāṉavaiyalla. ataṟku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiṉavai. māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟai yulakamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kāṇka. 
araṅkaṉ:- araṅkam - araṅkaṉ. araṅkam = āṟṟiṭaikkuṟai.
irāmaṉ:- irāmam - irāmaṉ. 
irāmam eṉpatu tirumaṇkāppu. irāmam = irēkai vari. irāmak karumpu eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkkuka. vaṭanāṭṭil itait tavaṟāka nāmam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. irāmar oru vaiṇava paktar. irāmarukku muṉpē māl vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatu. 
iraṇṭu, alampu, ilakku, ulāttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyirmutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkuvatupōla araṅkaṉ irāmaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷum uyir mutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nārāyaṇaṉ: nāram + ayaṉaṉ.
varuṇaṉ: vār + aṇam - vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ - varuṇaṉ.
vār + i = vāri. vār + iti = vāriti.
ātmā: āṉ + mā = āṉmā. āṉ = pacu. mā perumaip poruḷ  vikuti. māṉ eṉpatu īṟu kuṟaintu niṉṟateṉiṉum okkum. Gk. animos. vaṭamoḻiyil ātmā carīrattaik kuṟikkum.
naraṉ: narai + aṉ = naraṉ = naraippavaṉ.
pati: patikkut talaivaṉ;  iṭavāku peyar. pati = nakar, vīṭu.
pacu: pācattāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pacu.
pācam: pacumai - paccai - pāci - pācam. pācampōṟ piṇikkuṅ kayiṟu; kayiṟupōṟ piṇikkum malam.
pācam eṉpataṟku ācai yeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum poruntum.
narakam: narakal - narakam.
cf. dungeon, from dung.
kāyam - ākāyam. kāyam = karuppu. kāyap peyarvayiṉ eṉṟu tolkāppiyattuḷ varutal kāṇka.
pūtam: potu - potukku - pontu - pūtam.
ulakam: ula + ku = ulaku. ulaku + am = ulakam.
ulattal = aḻital.
nīr + am = nīram - nāram = nār + am.
cali + am = calam. cali + aṉam = calaṉam. cali + ati = calati. calittal = acaital. calippu eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkaiyum acaivu eṉṉum meyppāṭṭaiyum oppu nōkkuka.
kālam - kāl + am = kālam. camai + am = camaiyam. (viti mutaṉilait toḻilākupeyar.)

cf. maturity, from mature - to ripen.

c.f. Aurora Borealis, the northern light.
aurora - fire;  borealis - northern.

``kaṇkāl puṟamakam uḷḷuḻai kīḻmēl

iṉi, akkā, attā, āṇi, ammā, ampā, aṭavi, āḷi, kaṭu, kāvēri, kucam, kūccam, kuṭi, kūṉ, kuḷam, kōṭṭai, kaṭṭil, cām, cā, paṭṭaṇam, poṉ, paḷḷi, veḷ mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teṉcoṟkaḷām. iṉṉum ivai pōṉṟa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻivāyp paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaracu talaitāḻttapiṉ centamiḻārāycci cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka illāmaiyāṉ, vaṭamoḻip pulavar tamiḻarai oru peṭṭai miraṭṭu miraṭṭi vantaṉar. ippōtō moḻiyārāycciyāl eṇṇiṟanta vaṭamoḻi vāyccoṟkaḷ muṭavaṉ kutiraikaḷāyt tirumpi varukiṉṟaṉa. cila  tamiḻar vaṭamoḻi vāyppaṭṭa coṟkaḷai yaṟintiruntum  avaṟṟai eṭuttuk kūṟat tuṇintilar.
meyyuṭai yoruvaṉ colamāṭ ṭāmaiyāṟ
poypō lummē poypō lummē
vaṭanūlār tamiḻccoṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉkoṇṭapiṉ avaṟṟait  tamiḻ vaṭivil vaittiruppatillai. uṭaṉē urut teriyātu māṟṟi viṭuvar. maṟaikkāṭu, paḻamalai pōṉṟa iṭappeyarkaḷaikkūṭa vētāraṇyam, viruttācalam eṉṟu moḻipeyarppārāyiṉ vēṟu eṉ colvatu? ivvāṟu ettaṉaiyō teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟu vākukarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avai naḷarāṅ kālamum naṇṇum.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatellām vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟu moḻi nūluṇarātār cilar koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. mūla mutaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻ tavira vēṟu enta moḻiyāvatu piṟacoṟka ḷillātatillai. oru collai iṉṉa moḻiyeṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakutip poruḷaiyum ataṉiṉṟum tirinta palvēṟu coṟkaḷaiyum pārkkavēṇṭum. avai enta moḻiyiluḷḷaṉavō anta moḻikkē atu uriyatām. viṉaic collāyiṉ puṭaipeyarcci (Conjugation) vēṇṭum. peyarc collāyiṉ  bgaÇy¡fz«(Declension) vēṇṭum. kal eṉṉum viṉai  kaṟṟāṉ, kaṟṟāḷ, kaṟṟār, kaṟṟatu, kaṟṟaṉa, kaṟṟa, kaṟka, kaṟpittāṉ, kaṟkappaṭṭatu, kaṟṟilaṉ, kalai eṉap pala vaṭivukaḷilum puṭaipeyara vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyilō atu kalā  (fiy) eṉat toḻilākupeyarāy maṭṭum niṟkum. iḵtōr utāraṇam.
cila vaṭamoḻip paṟṟuṭaiyār muṉivar eṉṉum collukku muṉi eṉpatu pakutiyeṉ ṟaṟiyāmal, mavuṉam ceypavar muṉivar eṉṟu vaṭamoḻivaḻi yuraippar. aiyaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ muṟaippeyarai āriyaṉ eṉpatiṉ citaivākak kūṟuvar. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaic coṟkaḷukkum avar vaṭamoḻiyaik kaṟpippatuṇṭu.
oru vakuppārāvatu piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ iyaipiṉṟit tāmāy ulakattil vantavarallar. aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ tavira oru moḻiyāvatu piṟamoḻiyiṉ kalappiṉṟit tāṉāy vantataṉṟu eṉṟu teḷika.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' kaṭakam 1931
2
vaṭamoḻit teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām oru perun tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum utittavai yeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūl (History), kulanūl   (Ethnology), moḻinūl (Philology) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷālum  valiyuṟuttappaṭum. āyiṉum, ticaiccol vakaiyil oru vakuppār putiyaṉavāy ākkik koṇṭaṉavum,tāymoḻiyiṉiṉṟum putiyaṉavāyt tirittukkoṇṭaṉavumāṉa iruvakaic coṟkaḷ ovvoru moḻikkum ciṟappākum. iv viruvakaic coṟkaḷum  oru kiḷaimoḻikkuriya ataṉ tāymoḻiyaic cārum; oru tāymoḻik kuriyavai oru perun tāy moḻiyaic cārum.   tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai nōkkat tamiḻ oru tāymoḻi yum, āriya moḻikaḷai nōkkat tamiḻ oru perun tāymoḻiyumākum. eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamaiyālum, āriyac coṟkaḷil pala urutteriyātavāṟu attuṇait tirintuḷḷatiṉālum,  pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ciṟappuc coṟkaḷākavē tōṉṟāniṟkum. ēṉaic coṟkaḷai itupōtuḷḷa teṉcoṟ ṟuṇaikoṇṭē teṉcol leṉak kāṭṭal moḻinūlāṉ muṭṭiṉṟi muṭivatākum. iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷāyiṉ  iv varampiṟ kuṭpaṭā.
oru collai oru moḻik kuriyateṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakuti (stem) allatu mūlam (root) emmoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉṟu aṟital  vēṇṭum.
oru colliṉ pakuti allatu mūlam palamoḻiyi liruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil, pakuti iyaṟkaiyāṉateṉṟum, poruḷ ciṟantateṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
pala moḻiyilum, pakuti vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottiruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, avaiyum pala moḻiyiliruppiṉ, etil avai kaḻipalaveṉṟum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum.
ettuṇaiyō teṉcoṟkaḷai vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟu paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñaruṅkūṭap pakarntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu : 
akaṅkāram.  akam + kāram. akam - avvulakam, vāṉulaku, 
mōkṣam. akaraccuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟantatu; cēymaiyiluḷḷa vāṉulakattaik kuṟippatu; ampar eṉṉum coṟpōla. a - ampar - amparam. ikarac cuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟanta ikam, impar eṉṉum coṟkaḷ aṇmaic cuṭṭāy ivvulakattaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
viṇṇulakaiyum maṇṇulakaiyum cēymai aṇmaipaṟṟi avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpatu marapu.
``aruḷillārk kavvulaka millai poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku''	(kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum. 
vīṭu eṉṉum muttip peyar illattai uṇarttiṉāṟpōla, akam eṉṉum peyarum illattai uṇarttum. vīṭu = viṭutalai; viṭuvatu vīṭu. piṟavit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai ceyvatu pēriṉpa vīṭu. veyiṉ maḻait tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai ceyvatu ivvulaka vīṭu.
il eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyar ēḻām vēṟṟumai urupāy uṭpākattaik kuṟittāṟpōla, akam eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyarum uṭpākattaiyum uḷḷattaiyum kuṟikkum akameṉ kiḷavi eṉat taṉittamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyattuḷḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kāram:  kari eṉṉum viṉaiyaṭiyāp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar. kari  + am = kāram;  mutaṉilai tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
cf: paṭi + am = pāṭam. tavi + am = tāvam.
karittal mikutal. kāram mikuti.
kari eṉpatu kaṭi eṉṉum uriccolliṉ tiripu.
ṭa-ra, pōli. o.nō: paṭavar - paravar. mukaṭi - mukari.
uppukkarikkum, uppuk kaṭukkum eṉpaṉa oruporuḷpaṟṟiya ulaka vaḻakku. kaṭi eṉṉum uriccoṟ poruḷkaḷuḷ mikutiyum oṉṟu.
kāram eṉṉum col mutalāvatu mikutiyai uṇarttip piṉpu uṟaippu mikutiyai uṇarttum.
kari eṉṉum collē ati eṉṉum upacarkkam (prefix)  peṟṟu  atikari eṉa niṟkum. atikarippatu atikāram.
ati eṉpatu oru mikutip poruḷupacarkkam (intensive prefix). 
atikārattai (āṇai mikutiyai or āṟṟaṉ mikutiyai) uṭaiyavaṉ atikāri.
oru nūliṉ perumpākam atikāram.
akam karippatu akaṅkāram. akaṅkāram - āṅkāram.
akaṇṭam:  a + kaṇṭam. a, etirmaṟai upacarkkam; al eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. al eṉṉum etirmaṟaic collē un eṉṟu āriyamoḻikaḷil tiriyum. il eṉpatu il, in, im, eṉṟu tiriyum.
kaṇṭam : kaṇ - kaṇu - kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi. cf: tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi. taṇṭu - taṇṭi - taṭi.
kaṇpōṉṟu iruppatu kaṇu.
kaṇukkaṇuvāy naṟukkuvatu kaṇṭu.
kaṇṭu eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu kaṇṭi eṉṉum viṉaiyuṇṭākum. kaṇṭittal kaṇukkaṇuvāy allatu tuṇṭutuṇṭāy veṭṭutalum, aṅṅaṉam veṭṭutalpōṟ kaṭumoḻi kūṟalum.
kaṇṭippatu kaṇṭam. kaṇṭi + am = kaṇṭam. cf. tuṇṭi + am = tuṇṭam. taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam.
kaṇṭi eṉṉum col iṭaik kuṟaintu kaṭi eṉṟum niṟkum. cf. taṇṭi - taṭi.
kaṇṭam eṉpataṉ nīṭṭal kāṇṭam. cf. karaṇam - kāraṇam.
kaṇṭam = tuṇṭu, pākam, pirivu.
kaṇṭam eṉpatu cataippākam nilappākaṅkaḷaiyum, kāṇṭam (canto) eṉpatu nūṟpākaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
Canto eṉṉum āṅkilac collai cano(to sing) eṉṉum ilattīṉ      viṉaiyaṭip piṟappākak koḷvatu ciṟantataṉṟu.
akati: a - al eṉpataṉ kaṭaikkuṟai.
kati - celavu, kaṭa eṉpataṉ tiripu.
katittal - cellutal. kati + am = katam.
irākkatam - irāc celavu.
irākkataṉ - irācceṉṟu uṇavu tērpavaṉ.
irākkataṉ - rākṣasaṉ (Transliteration)
		nicācaraṉ (Translation)
		nici = iravu; caram - acaivu, celavu.
akantai: akam - akantai,  akattiṉ mikuti. 
akam = maṉam. cf. kuṭam - kuṭantai    (kumpakōṇam). uṭam - uṭantai. cf. uḷḷam = ūkkam, uḷḷattiṉ mikuti.
akarāti: akaram + āti. akaram - karam cāriyai.
āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭumuṉṉum, tamiḻil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaḻinūlkaḷē akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum. karam, kāram mutaliya eḻuttuc cāriyaikaḷ mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaṭkillai. āti potuccol.
akāraṇam:  a + kāraṇam.
karaṇam - kāraṇam. karu + aṇam = karaṇam.
karuttal - mēkam karuttalaik kuṟikkum.
karu - kār. mēkam cūl koḷḷumpōtu karuttiruttaliṉ makkaḷiṉ cūlum karu, karuppam eṉappaṭum.
mēkam karuttu maḻaipoḻivatu ulaka viṉaikaṭkellām kāraṇamā yiruttaliṉ karu eṉpatu kāraṇattaiyum kuṟikkum.

karaṇam, karuvi eṉpaṉa karu eṉṉum oruviṉait toḻiṟ peyarkaḷ. tirikaraṇam = mukkaruvi. antakkaraṇam - uṭkaruvi. karaṇam aṇam vikuti. karuvi - vi vikuti. karaṇattiṉ nīṭṭam kāraṇam. karuvi mēkattiṉ tokutiyaiyum uṇarttum; e-ṭu: karuvi māmaḻai, karuvi tokuti - tolkāppiyam.  karu - kār.  kār + aṇam = kāraṇam.  kār + iyam = kāriyam, iyam toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
akālam. a + kālam.
kālam: kāl+am=kālam. am cāriyai. kāl uṭampiṉ kāṟpākamākiya uṟuppu.

acaram. a + caram.
cari + am = caram. toḻiṟpeyar.

acalam. a + calam = acalam = malai, acaiyātatu.
cali + am = calam, toḻiṟpeyar.
calittal - acaital.
calam = nīr, calippatu, toḻilāku peyar.
calam eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭil nīrpporuḷkaḷil oṉṟākiya cīḻukku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
calam eṉṉum teṉcollaiyē jalam eṉat tirittaṉar āriyarum tamiḻarum.
`jeṭṭiyār kaṭaikku jeṉṟu jēlam jīṉi vāṅki vā' eṉṟu iṉṟaikkum tamiḻmakkaḷ teṉcoṟkaḷai vaṭacoṟpōlat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. calippu eṉpatu kaḷaippu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭāṟ pōla, ataṉ pariyāyac collākiya acaivu eṉpatum kaḷaippu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.

``eḻuvā yurupu tiripil peyarē'' eṉṉum cūttirattil, urupu eṉpatu vaṭiveṉṟē poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.

3
vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkun
tolkāppiyat teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉareṉṟu carittira ārāycciyāl teḷivākiṉṟatu. tamiḻar, teluṅkar, karunaṭar,  malaiyāḷar, tuḷuvar mutaliya tirāviṭa vakuppiṉarellārum eṅṅaṉam  oru kālattil oru vakuppiṉarā yiruntaṉarō,   aṅṅaṉamē turēṉiyar, cēmiyar, āriyar eṉṉum mupperuṅ kiḷaiyiṉarum oru kālattil orē vakuppiṉarā yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vakupput tiriyat tiriya avarkaḷ  moḻiyun tirintukoṇṭē vantirukkiṉṟatu. paṭaippuk kālantoṭṭu oru vakuppār piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ kalavātu pirintiruntāloḻiya avarkaḷ moḻi taṉimoḻiyā yiruttal eṭṭuṇaiyum kūṭātatākum. peṟṟōr moḻikaḷaiyē piḷḷaikaḷum pēcukiṉṟaṉar. uṟaviṉarellārum oru moḻiyē pēcutal iyalpu. ākavē, iyaipuṭaiya vakuppiṉa rellām iyaipuṭaiya moḻikaḷaiyē pēcal vēṇṭum. camaṟkiruta āriyar mūvakai moḻikaḷum kalakkiṉṟa mēlaiyāciyāviṉ vaṭapuṟattil ātiyiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. aṅkiruntu avarkaḷ vaṭaintiyāviṟku varumuṉṉarē avarkaḷ moḻiyil piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa kalantuviṭṭaṉa. āriya varukaippōtu intu tēya muḻutum vaḻaṅki vantavai tiruntiṉavum tiruntātaṉavumāṉa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷē. āriyarkaḷ vaṭa intiyāvi lirukkumpōtu pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ vēṟu pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷum camaṟkirutattiṟ kalantuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻkkalvi talaitāḻttuc carittira aṟivum moḻinūlārāycciyum illāta iṭaikkālattu, camaṟkirutamē intiya moḻikaṭ kellām tāy eṉṉumōr paṭiṟṟurai tamiḻp pakaiñarāl tamiḻnāṭṭilum parappappaṭṭatu. camaṟkiruta akarātiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām camaṟkirutamē yeṉṉuṅ kuruṭṭu nampikkai tamiḻaruḷḷattilum kuṭikoṇṭatu. mēṉāṭṭuk kalaiccuṭar mēmpaṭṭut tikaḻum ikkālattuṅkūṭap pala tūya  teṉcoṟkaḷai vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar pēraṟiñarāyiṉārum. tamiḻ, ulakat tāymoḻiyiṉ iyaṟkai vaṭivum camaṟkirutam ataṉ ceyaṟkai vaṭivumātaliṉ,  av viru moḻikkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷuḷ, tamiḻil mūlapporuḷ taruvaṉa vellām tamiḻeṉṟē perumpāluṅ koḷḷaṟpālaṉa. camaṟkirutam eṉṉum peyarē naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutaliṉ camaṟkirutattiṉ ceyaṟkaiyai yuṇarttum.
tamiḻiṟ piṟkālattu vaṭacoṟkaḷ perituṅ kalantiruppiṉum paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷ attuṇaik kalappiṉavalla. kālattiṟ piṉṉōkkic cellac cellat tamiḻ nūlkaḷil vaṭacol vaḻakku arukik koṇṭē cellum. iṟṟai muḻuttamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ muṉṉaiyatu tolkāppiyamātaliṉ atil vantuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ ācai, vaiciyar pōṉṟa aintāṟē yākum. vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkum ataṉ piṟa coṟka ḷellām tūya teṉcollē yātal veḷḷiṭaimalai. āyiṉum ārāycci yillārkku aṟivippāṉ vēṇṭi avaṟṟai muṟaiyē ovvoṉṟā yeṭuttu moḻinūṟpaṭi viḷakkuvām.

4
`ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṇam'
vaṭamoḻiyum teṉmoḻiyum eṉatu uṉatu eṉṟu, eṅkun toṭarn tetirvaḻakkāṭaṟ kiṭamāṉa eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷ, `ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṇam' eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām.
ulakil aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻum makkaḷ tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum poruḷkaḷai māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻum vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tammuṭ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷai māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷum tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum coṟkaḷai māṟṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, ulakamoḻikaḷellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉaveṉṟu moḻi nūl vallār kaṇṭa muṭiviṟkēṟpa, aṭuttu vaḻaṅkātu neṭṭiṭaiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaṭkum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ potuvāyiruttalai ārāycciyāṟ kāṇalām. āṅkilam ēṟattāḻa 6,000 mailkaṭkappāl vaḻaṅkiṉum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷait taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟiṉ virivaiyellām eṉatu moḻinūluṭ (Evolution of Language: Vol.1. The Primitive Language and the PreAryan Tamils) kaṇṭukoḷka. utāraṇamākac cila coṟkaḷai īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum.

iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa iṉṟiyamaiyāta coṟkaḷai akarāti muṟaiyiṉum, kalaimuṟaiyiṉum, eṉatu moḻinūluḷ eṭuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṉṟām. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
ic coṟkaḷ ellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟum tamiḻukku vantavai yalla. āṅkilam varumuṉṉarē ivai tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. āṟāyiram kalliṭaiyiṭṭa āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum ittuṇaip potuc coṟkaḷirukkumāyiṉ, ēṟattāḻa 5,000 āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum ettuṇaip potuc coṟkaḷiruttal vēṇṭum? ip potuccoṟkaḷellām moḻi nūṉ muṟaiyil kūrntu nōkkuvārkkut tūya teṉcoṟkaḷeṉṟē tōṉṟā niṟkum, vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvāyuḷḷa coṟkaḷai, vaṭacolleṉpārum teṉ colleṉpārumām irucārār moḻi nūlaṟiñar. vaṭamoḻiyaip pōṉṟē teṉmoḻiyum ciṟantatoru tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉmoḻikku vantuḷḷa coṟkaḷaip pōlavē teṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷum paṉṉūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu `ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṉam' eṉpaṉa.
1. āriyar vaṭaintiyāviṟku varumuṉṉarē (B.C. 3000) tamiḻil ilakkaṇamiruntamaikku, 
``kaḷaviṉuṅ kaṟpiṉuṅ kalakka millāt
talaivaṉun talaiviyum pirinta kālaik
.....     .......             .......         .........
uḷḷuṟut tiṟiṉē yuyarkaḻi yāṉantap
paiyu ḷeṉṟu paḻittaṉar pulavar''	(yāpparuṅkalavirutti mēṟkōḷ)
eṉṟu akattiyam vaḻinūṉ muṟaimaiyil āciriya vacaṉaṅ kūṟuvatē cāṉṟām. mēlum, eḻuttilakkaṇam, collilaṇakkam, yāppilakkaṇa meṉṟa mūṉṟumē ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkiya miyampalākat tamiḻ pōlumoru ceyyuḷ moḻikku mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ taṉ nuṇṇaṟivāl vakuttiruttal kūṭum. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi ēṉaimoḻikaṭ kellāmillāta poruḷilakkaṇamum, icait tamiḻ nāṭakat tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, āciriyarākat tamiḻarāka oruvarē oruṅkēyiyaṟṟiṉar eṉpatu eṅṅaṉ poruntum? akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇamaṉṟē? ātalāṉ muttami ḻilakkaṇam oruvarāṉaṉṟip palarāṉ muṟaiyē neṭṭiṭai yiṭṭiṭṭē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
akattiyar potiyañ ceṉṟatum, murukaṉpāṟ cuvaṭi peṟṟatumāṉa purāṇak kataikaḷum tamiḻnūliṉ toṉmutu paḻaimaiyai uṇarttuvaṉa vākum.
akattiyarum tolkāppiyarum mutaṉūlāciriyaraik kuṟikku miṭattu, yāṇṭum palarpālākavē kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. āriyar varaviṟku muṉ tamiḻilakkaṇa miruntiruppiṉ ataṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷum tamiḻiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. tamiḻil iyal eṉṉum col ilakkaṇattaik kuṟippiṉum, atu ataiyē varaiyaṟuttuc cuṭṭāmaiyāṉum, kuṇam, iyaṟkai eṉap palaporuḷ paṭutalāṉum, iyaṟcol, iyaṟpeyar, iyaṉmoḻi, iyaṟpaṭa moḻital mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷil ilakkaṇattilum vēṟāka vevvēṟu poruḷ tarutalāṉum, muttamiḻil ovvoṉṟum ilakkaṇam ilakkiya meṉa iruvakaippaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟaik kuṟikka vēṟu colliṉmai yālum, iyal eṉpatu muttamiḻil oṉṟaṉ ilakkaṇamu milakkiyamumāta lāṉum `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tamiḻukkiṉṟi yamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉavām.
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntami ḻēṉai
maṇṇi ṭaiccila vilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu tamiḻp pulavar tarukkumāṟu `attuṇai nirampiya vilakkaṇattai yuṭaiya tamiḻmoḻiyil, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟikkac collillaiyeṉiṉ atu eṭṭuṇaiyum nampaṟkiṭaṉākātu.
vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattaik kuṟikka viyākaraṇameṉṟōr taṉic colluḷatu. teṉmoḻiyil ilakkaṇattait tavira vēṟoṉṟumilatu. ēṉai moḻikaṭkellā millāta aintilakkaṇam nirampiya taṉit tāy moḻikku ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cuṭṭac collillā tirukkumō?
3. `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum, `lakṣaṇam, `lakṣyam' eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷum muṟaiyē vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.
	tamiḻ	camakirutam
ilakkaṇam =	1. Grammar 	lakṣaṇam =	1. Grammar
	2. aḻaku		2. aḻaku
	3. Definition		3. Definition
	4. kuṇam		4. kuṇam
	5. oḻuṅku		5. oḻuṅku
ilakkiyam =	1. Literature	lakṣyam =	1. Literature
	2. tiruṣṭāntam		2. tiruṣṭāntam
	3. poruḷ		3. poruḷ 
lakṣyampaṇṇavillai - poruṭpaṭuttavillai.
ākavē, ic colliṇaikaḷ oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟē tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum. eccolliṉiṉṟum eccolleṉpatē kūṭā. itukāṟum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilellām, `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉumiraṇṭum lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripeṉṟē kūṟap paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. nūlāciriyarum uraiyāciriyarumāṉa piṟṟai yilakkaṇi kaḷuṭ perumpālār vaṭanūl vaḻiyarātalāṉum, akkālattu moḻi yārāycciyiṉmaiyāṉum, avar vaṭanūlvaḻi kūṟiyatellām maṟuppiṉṟi vaḻivaḻi vaḻuvappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum ēṉai moḻikaḷellām ērāḷamākavum tārāḷamākavum kaṭaṉ koḷḷu meṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl vaḻakkiṟkum moḻipeyarppiṟkum pōtiya coṉṉirampāveṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyoṉṟē piṟamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu oru collum kaṭaṉ koḷḷātu taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭaiyateṉṟum oru tavaṟāṉa koḷkai, iṉṟum cila vaṭamoḻiyāḷark kiruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻittuṇai ciṟitumiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭaiya teṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar kūṟiyiruppavum, ataṟku māṟāka vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkal tamiḻkkuc cālāteṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷaraṉṟic tamiḻppulavar cilaruṅkūṭat talaivirittāṭu kiṉṟaṉar. (vaṭamoḻivāṇar tammaip pukaḻak karutiyō, allatu vaṭamoḻivāṇarākat tammaik kāṭṭak karutiyō terikilam.) tamiḻar moḻinūlēyaṟiyā reṉṟum cuyamāṉa teṉcoṟkum vaṭacoṉ mūlaṅka kaṟpippatē avarkaḷukkup perumaiyeṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār (Bishop Caldwell) kūṟiyiruppatu iṉṟum meyyāyiruppatu peritum iraṅkattakka toṉṟām. tamiḻāḷar cilar teṉcoṟka ḷiṉṉiṉṉaveṉṟu aiyantiripaṟa vuṇarntiruntum avaṟṟai veḷippaṭuttum uṟutiyiṉṟi, vaṭamoḻiyar vāymaruṭṭuk kañci, ``viṇpaṭṭa kokku vallūṟu kaṇṭaṉṉa'' vilavilakkiṉṟamaiyiṉ avar kāṭci ūmaiyaṉ kaṇṭa kaṉāppōla oruciṟitum payaṉpaṭātu oḻikiṉṟatu.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam'  eṉṟa coṟkaṭkut teṉcol vaṭivamē iyaṟkaiyākavum vaṭacol vaṭivam ceyaṟkaiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru col oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟum maṟṟōr moḻikkuc ceṉṟateṉpataṟku ataṉ pakutipporuḷ allatu mūlapporuḷē ātāram. utāraṇamāka venna eṉṟa teluṅkuc coṟku mūlap poruḷiṉmaiyiṉ atu veḷḷai neyyeṉap poruḷpaṭum veṇṇey eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripeṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. emmoḻiyil atu peruvaḻakkō, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷ (Derivatives) peruntokaiyaṉavō, ammoḻikkē aḵtu uritteṉat tuṇiyappaṭum.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pakutip poruḷilatu; teṉmoḻiyilō teḷivāyuḷatu. yātōveṉiṟ kūṟutum.
1. ilakku eṉṉuñ col mutaṉmutal ilaiyai uṇarttum. ōrilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu. īrilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu nellai vaḻakku.
2.  iraṇṭāvatu kuṟiyai uṇarttum, paṇṭaikkālattil, vilvīrar oru marattiluḷḷa ōrilaiyait tēṭi ilakkāka vaittu eytu payiṉ ṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāñcāli maṇattiṉum ilakkaṇai maṇattiṉum, ilakkāka iṭappaṭṭa maccam ilaivaṭivāyiruttal kāṇka. ikkālattum kalleṟivallār ōr marattiṉuḷḷa kāykaḷaiyē kuṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil vilvīrar ilaikaḷaik kuṟik koṇṭamai itaṉālum anumikkappaṭum. ilaiyē kāyiṉum ciṟitāyum nilaiyāṉatāyu miruttal kāṇka.
3. mūṉṟāvatu kuṟikkappaṭṭa ōr iṭattai uṇarttum. atukālai lekku eṉṟu tiriyum, iraṇṭu, reṇṭu eṉṟāṟ pōla, L. locus, a place, local, locality, location  mutaliya coṟkaḷ itaṉiṉṟum piṟakkum. lekku eṉṉuñ col teṉṉāṭṭiṟ peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
4. nāṉkāvatu kuṟikkappaṭṭa utāraṇattai uṇarttum
5. aintāvatu cittirattai uṇarttum. makkaḷ eḻuttaṟiyumuṉṉarē ōviyam aṟintiruntaṉar. aḻaku aṉaivar kaṇkaḷaiyum kavartaliṉ, āṭai, cuvar mutaliyavaṟṟil, ilai koṭi mutaliya pala vaṭivukaḷai yeḻutivantaṉar.
``māṭakkuc cittiram'' eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.  ettuṇaiyō aṟivu vaḷarcci peṟṟa ikkālattum maranuṇviṉai (Wood Carving), ulōka nuṇviṉai (Engraving), ōviyam (Drawing), kaṇṇuḷviṉai (Painting)  mutaliya cittira vēlaikaḷellām marañceṭi, koṭi, ilai, pū, kāy, kaṉiyākavē yiruttal kāṇka. āṭaikaḷiṉ karaikaḷellām perumpālum tāvara vaṭiviṉavē. itaṉāṟ cittiram ilaittoḻileṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ilaiyeṉpatu ēṉai yuṟuppukaṭkum iṉavilakkaṇam (upalakṣaṇam). ``ilaimukap paimpūṇ'' eṉṟār kumarakuruparar. ulakap poruḷkaḷil tāvaram pōl kaṇkavar kaviṉpeṟu kāṭciyaṉa vēṟevaiyumillai. itaṉālēyē koṭi, koṭiyiṭai, kulakkoṭi, kompu, kōtai, kulāmālai, malarmukam eṉa mātar pukaḻappaṭuvatum, tiruviḻākkaḷil tōraṇam pantar mutaliyavai tāvarap poruḷkaḷāl puṉaiyappaṭuvatu meṉka.
ilaṅkum eṉṉum viṉai, cittirattai yuṇarttum ilakku eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟum tirintatē.
ilaṅkutal viḷaṅkutal, ilakkam viḷakkam.
6. āṟāvatu cittiraveḻuttai (Hieroglyph or Picture Writing) yuṇarttum. itupōtuḷḷa variyeḻuttēṟpaṭumuṉ ellā nāṭukaḷilum cittira veḻuttiḷ mūlamāyē makkaḷ tattam karuttai veḷiyiṭṭu vantaṉar. oru cittiram oru vākkiyattaiyāvatu oru ceytiyaiyāvatu kuṟippā luṇarttuvatāyiruntatu.
7. ēḻāvatu variyeḻuttai yuṇarttum. cittiraveḻuttut tōṉṟic ciṉṉūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉ variyeḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. av variyeḻuttum kūrntu nōkkiṉ tāvara vaṭivē tāṅki niṟkum. cappāṉiyam, cīṉam mutaliya moḻiyeḻuttukaḷ ikkālattum cittira vaṭivē peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
eḻututal eṉṉum viṉai paṭam varaitalaiyum eḻutteḻutu talaiyum potuvāyuṇarttum.
eḻuttu ilakku (ilai) vaṭivāyiruttaliṉ eḻututal ilakkitta leṉappaṭṭatu.
``ivvuruvu neñceṉṉuṅ kiḻiyiṉmē viruntilakkit 
tavvuruvu niṉaippeṉṉun tukilikaiyāl varuttittu''	(cīvaka. 180)
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil ilakkittu eṉpatu eḻuti eṉṉum poruḷil vantuḷḷatu. iv viṉaiyiṉiṉṟē lēkakaṉ, lēkiṉi, likitam mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
ilakku, ilakki eṉpaṉa eḻutu eṉap poruḷpaṭum viṉaip pakutikaḷ allatu ēval viṉaikaḷām.
ilakku eṉpatu mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarākavumirukkum; atu ākupeyarāy eḻuttai yuṇarttum.
Gk. logos = a word
	  a discourse
	  a treatise
	  a science
e.g: Theology, Physiology
ilakkiyam eṉpatu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. ilakku pakuti; iyam vikuti.
cf.	kaṇ + iyam = kaṇṇiyam.
	kaṇṇutal = karututal, matittal
ilakkiyam eṉpatu toḻilāku peyarāy eḻuttaiyum, karuviyāku peyarāy nūlkaḷaiyum uṇarttum, nūluṇarttuḻi irumaṭiyāku peyar.
cf. Literature from letter.
ilakkiyattil iyam eṉṉum vikutiyait taṉiccollākkic cāttirameṉap poruḷ kūṟal īṇṭup poruntātu.
iṉi, ilakkaṇam eṉṉum collum ōr vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyarēyām. ilakku pakuti. aṇam vikuti.
cf. kaṭṭu + aṇam = kaṭṭaṇam
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum mutaṉmutal, eḻutum toḻilai yuṇarttip piṉpu ākupeyarāy eḻuttai yuṇarttum. ilakkiyam eṉpatu eḻuttālāya nūṟṟokutiyai yuṇarttiṉāṟpōla, ilakkaṇameṉpatu eḻuttālāya moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkai yuṇarttum.
cf. Grammar from gramnos, (Gk.) a letters. 
iṉi, ilakkaṇameṉpatu maṟṟōr vikutit toḻiṟ peyar. ilakku pakuti; am vikuti. eṇ ilakka vaṭivāy atāvatu eḻuttu vaṭivā yiruttaliṉ ilakkameṉappaṭṭatu.

Logic from Logos (Gk)
``ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar eḻutteṉpariṉ pulavōr'' eṉṉumaṭiyil, eṇ tarukkattaiyum eḻuttu ilakkiyattaiyum (Literature) uṇarttutal kāṇka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum ilakku eṉṉum pakutiyaṭit toḻiṟpeyarkaḷeṉṟum, avai ākupeyar muṟaiyāṉ muṟaiyē nūṟṟokutiyaiyum moḻi yoḻuṅkaiyu muṇarttumeṉṟum, ilakku eṉṉuñ coṟku mutaṟporuḷ ilaiyum vaḻipporuḷ eḻuttu mutaliyaṉavumeṉṟum, ivaiyellān tamiḻēyeṉṟum, āriyat tamiḻk kalappiṉ piṉ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum āriyañ ceṉṟa eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷ ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭeṉṟum, ivai lakṣyam lakṣaṇameṉa āṇṭut tiriyumeṉṟum, iṉṉum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum veḷḷiṭaimalai pōṟ ṟeḷḷitiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
kutirai tiruṭiṉāṉ oruvaṉ, nītipati muṉ kutirai mukattai mūṭi itaṟku ekkaṇ kuruṭeṉṟa uṭaiyāṟku, iṭakkaṇ kuruṭeṉṟu akappaṭṭāṟ pōla, eṇṇiṟanta teṉcoṟkaḷaik kavarntukoṇṭa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, ic coṟku mūlameṉṉai yeṉṟōr tamiḻaṉ kaṭāva, itaṟku mūlamillai, iḵtō riṭukuṟiyeṉ ṟakappaṭṭuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
iṭukuṟippeyarē tamiḻkkillai.
``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē''
``moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā''
eṉpavai tolkāppiyam.
iṭukuṟip peyar vaṭamoḻiyil ērāḷam. pakutiyaṟiyappaṭāta teṉcoṟkaḷaiyellām kaṇṇai mūṭikkoṇṭu iṭukuṟiyeṉap pakarntuviṭuvar.
kutirai tiruṭiṉāṉ kutiraiyiṭam ceṉṟapōtu, atu uṭai yāṉaik kaṇṭāṟpōṉ makiḻāmaipōl, vaṭanūlār cila teṉcoṟkup pakutipporuḷ kaṟpikkiṉum atu ciṟitum poruntā tākiṉṟatu.
ākavē, moḻinūlārāycci vaṭacollum teṉcollum pakuttuṇartaṟkuc ciṟantatoru karuviyām. itaṉaiyaṟintu kaṭaippiṭikka.
- ``tamiḻppoḻil'' viṭai 1934
5
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam'
emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
tamiḻiṉ tāymaiyai vaṭavar neṭuṅkālamāy maṟaittuḷḷamai kāraṇamāka, ovvoru tamiḻccollaiyum tamiḻccol leṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku arumpāṭu paṭavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. aṅṅaṉam arumpāṭupaṭ ṭāyntu takka cāṉṟum ētuvuṅ koṇṭu nāṭṭiyum, atai nampāta perumai tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē uḷḷatu.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa iru collum teṉcollē yeṉṟu pallāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē poḻilil takka cāṉṟukāṭṭi niṟuviyirukkiṉṟēṉ. āyiṉum, aṟivāṟṟaliṉmaiyālō, aḻukkāṟ ṟoṭukūṭiya cerukkiṉālō, palar atai uṇarntatākat teriyavillai. ātalāl, ataṉai mīṇṭum mika viḷakkamākat terivikkat tuṇintēṉ.
cilar, ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarākavum tamiḻaṉparākavu miruntum, tām vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟuḷḷamai kāraṇamākat takaiyaṟat tarukki, yāṉ kaṇṭa moḻinūṉ muṭivukaḷai nampātatōṭu tākkiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyaṟivu vēṟu, moḻinūlaṟivu vēṟu eṉpatai iṉimēlāyiṉum avar uṇar vārāka.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa iru coṟkaḷum teṉcollā eṉṟu ārāyumuṉ, avaṟṟāl kuṟikkappeṟum iraṇṭum teṉmoḻiyi luṇṭā eṉṟu kāṇalvēṇṭum. ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntuvaruvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, mutaṉ mutalākavum avai nāvalantēyattil eḻunta moḻi teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu mikaiyaṉṟu. ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntiruppiṉ, avaṟṟaik kuṟikkac collum tamiḻil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa irucol tavira vēṟu teṉcolluṇṭā veṉiṉ, etuvu millai.
cilar, ilakkiyattiṟkuc `ceyyuḷ' eṉpatum, ilakkaṇattiṟku `nūl' eṉpatum tamiḻppeyar eṉpar. ceyyuḷ eṉpatu yāppu vaṭiviṟkup peyar eṉpatait tolkāppiyar yāppilakkaṇaviyalukkuc ceyyuḷiyal eṉṟu peyariṭṭataiyum, av viyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvē māttirai mutaliya      26 uṟuppukaḷaiyum, ammai mutaliya eṭṭoṭu kūṭṭi,
``nallicaip pulavar ceyyuḷ uṟuppeṉa
vallitiṟ kūṟi vakutturait taṉarē''
eṉṟu kūṟuvataiyum, 127ām, 128ām nūṟpākkaḷ ceviyuṟaiyum aṅkatamum paṟṟivarum ceyyuḷkaḷai muṟaiyē ceviyuṟaic ceyyuḷ eṉṟum, aṅkatac ceyyuḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvataiyum,
``eḻunilat teḻunta ceyyuḷ teriyiṉ'' 	(162)
``ceyyuḷ moḻiyāl cīrpuṉaintu yāppiṉ'' 	(234)
``ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa nāṭi'' 	(241)
eṉṟa piṟaviṭattellām `ceyyuḷ' eṉṉum col yāppu vaṭivattaiyē kuṟippataiyum,
``pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu eṉmaṉār pulavar'' 	(78)
eṉa yāppu eṉṟu muṉṉark kūṟiyataiyē,
``eḻunilat teḻunta ceyyuḷ teriyiṉ''
eṉac ceyyuḷ eṉṟu piṉṉar āciriyar peyar māṟṟik kūṟiyataiyum, nūlukkurai ceyyuḷ urainaṭai ākiya iru vaṭivilum paṇṭaik kālat tiruntatiṉāl avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāṉa ceyyuḷ vaṭivuraiyē eḻunilattuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa teṉpataiyum, ceyyuḷ urainaṭai ākiya iraṇṭaiyum taḻuviyatē ilakkiyam eṉpataiyum nōkkik kāṇpārāka. kāviyam (Epic) tamiḻil toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭṭataiyum kāṇka.
ceyyuḷaik kuṟittaṟku yāppu, pā, pāṭṭu mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷu muṇṭēyeṉiṉ, avaiyellām parupporuḷil oruporuṭ collāyiṉum nuṇporuḷil vēṟupaṭṭavai eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum. av vēṟupāṭāvaṉa:
yāppu = metrical composition
ceyyuḷ = poem, poetry
pā = variety of metre.
pāṭṭu = ode
tūkku = metrical rhythm.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭa ceyyuḷaiyum (Poem), ceyyuṭ ṭokutiyaiyum (Poetry), ceyyuḷ naṭaiyaiyum kuṟikkum.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu ceyyappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷaiyuṭaiyatu. cey, pakuti; uḷ, vikuti.
``vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ''
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
poem allatu poetry eṉṉum āṅkilaccollum itē poruḷaik koṇṭiruppatu viyakkattakkatu.
E. poem, (from) F. poeme, (from) L. poema, (from).
Gk. poema - poiema - poieo = make.
Gk. poiesis - poesis = making, poetry, E. poesy.
Gk. poietes - poetes = maker, poet, E. poet.
iṉi, nūl eṉṉuñ collai ārāyvām. nūl eṉpatu, oru moḻiyiṉ allatu kalaiyiṉ allatu vēṟētēṉumoru poruḷiṉ oḻuṅkai allatu iyalpai eṭuttuk kūṟum puttakattaiyum, ataṉ piṉpu ākupeyarp poruḷil atupaṟṟiya kalaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi, av voḻuṅkai allatu iyalpaiyē kuṟikkātu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, oru moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkaippaṟṟi viṉaviṉāl, ataṉ ilakkaṇameṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟaiyākumēyaṉṟi, ataṉ nūl eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟaiyākātu. atu vaḻakku maṉṟu. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu grammar allatu definition eṉṟum, nūl eṉpatu treatise eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu ilakkaṇa nūlaiyum, piṉṉatu oru nūlāl allatu nūṟṟokutiyāl uṇarttappeṟum aṟivuk kalaiyaiyum (Science) kuṟikkumpōtu ākupeyarām. logos eṉṉum kirēkkaccol, mutalāvatu collaiyē kuṟittup piṉṉar āṅkilattil uraiyāṭṭaiyum nūlaiyum kalaiyaiyum kuṟittamai kāṇka. āyiṉ, ip piṟporuḷkaḷil atu logue eṉṟum logg eṉṟum tirivatuṭaṉ, tokaiccoṟkaḷiṉ īṟākavum (suffix) māṟiviṭum. ilakkaṇam eṉṉum col nūlaik kuṟikkumpōtu moḻiyilakkaṇa muṭpaṭṭa ellāk kalaiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum kūṟum puttakaṅkaṭkellām potuppeyar eṉṟum vēṟupāṭu uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, orucilar pulam, iyal eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil ilakkaṇattaik kuṟikkum eṉpar.
pulam eṉṉum colliṉ iyaṟporuḷ aṟivu eṉpatē. iccolliṉ aṭip piṟanta pulamai pulavar eṉṟa coṟkaḷum, aṟivuṭaimai aṟiñar eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. ittakaiya potuccol moḻiyilakkaṇattaic ciṟappākac cuṭṭutaṟkup poruntātu.
``pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval''
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyiruppiṉum, atu piṟarāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷap paṭṭatum peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiyatum illai. aṟivunūlkaḷellā vaṟṟaiyum potuvākap pulam eṉpatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkam. ``pulampuri yantaṇar'' eṉṉum paripāṭaṟṟoṭaril (6 : 45) maṟainūl pulam eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ōr ākupeyar. vētam eṉṉum vaṭacollukkum aṟivu eṉpatē iyaṟporuḷākum. [É¤(=m¿.) - ntj«]. paṉampāraṉār kūṟṟil uḷḷa pulam eṉṉuñ col, potuvāka aṟivu allatu aṟivunūl ellāvaṟṟaiyum kuṟippatāyiṉum, iṭam nōkkic ciṟappāka ilakkaṇattai allatu ilakkaṇa nūlaik kuṟippatākak koḷḷappaṭum. āṉāl, itu āṭcipaṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi iyalpupaṟṟiyataṉṟu. itaṉāl pulam eṉpatu ilakkaṇap peyarākātu.
iṉi, iyal eṉṟa collōveṉiṉ, atu ellāp poruḷukkum uriya taṉmaiyai allatu iyalpaip potuppaṭaiyākac cuṭṭuvataṉṟi vēṟoṉṟum kuṟiyāmaiyiṉ, ilakkaṇap peyarātaṟku eḷḷaḷavum ēṟṟataṉṟām. iyal = taṉmai. iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu = oruvaṉuṭaiya ciṟanta taṉmaikaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟip pukaḻvatu. iyal = iyalpu, iyaṟkai.
piṟa moḻikaḷil ilakkaṇam tōṉṟu muṉpum, āriyar varumuṉpum toṉṟutoṭṭum, tamiḻil ilakkaṇa, ilakkiyam iruntuvaruvatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum, tamiḻiltāṉ, piṟa moḻikaḷukkellā millāta poruḷilakkaṇamum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. itaṉālēyē, 
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntaip pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu parinturaittār parañcōti muṉivar,
iṉi, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭamoḻi vaṭivāṉa lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉpavaṟṟiṟku vaṭamoḻiyil, grammar, literature eṉṉum poruḷuṇṭā veṉiṉ, atuvumillai. vaṭamoḻiyil avaṟṟiṉ poruḷāvaṉa:
lakṣaṇa = kuṟi, aṭaiyāḷam, iyalpu, taṉmai, kuṇam, aḻaku, (mutaliyaṉa).
lakṣyam = kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatu, varaṇikkap paṭṭatu, kuṟikkōḷ, nōkku, kuṟikkōḷuṭaiyatu (mutaliyaṉa).
iv viru coṟkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaik karuttum kuṟi eṉpatēyākum. āṉāl, ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam, uŠ¢(ranj) eṉpataṉ tirivāṉa rakta (rakta) eṉṟum, lak (lag) eṉṟum, iruvēṟu vakaiyākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
rañc = varaṇam pūcappeṟu, niṟaṅkoḷ, viḷaṅku, oḷir.
ic col raṅ (rang) eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
lak = icai, iṇai, kaṭṭu, oṭṭu.
ic col, lego eṉṉum kirēkkac collukkum, ligne eṉṉum ilattīṉ collukkum iṉac collākum.
lakṣaṇa, lakṣya eṉṉum coṟkaḷuṭaṉ, lakṣam (nūṟāyiram), lakṣmaṉ allatu lakṣmaṇaṉ, lakṣmī mutaliya coṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil toṭarpuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. lakṣmaṉ, lakṣmaṇaṉ=ciṟanta kuṟikaḷai allatu nallilakkaṇaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉ. lakṣmī = naṟkuṟiyai allatu atirṣṭattai yuṭaiyavaḷ;  atirṣṭacāli allatu teyvam.
lakṣmī eṉṉum collukkuc cilar lak (lag) eṉpataiyum, cilar lañc (lanj) eṉpataiyum, mūlamākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. lañc = oḷir, viḷaṅku, tikaḻ.
lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātatōṭu, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷaippōla, kuṟipporuḷaiyē aṭippaṭaiyākavum, viḷaṅkutaṟ poruḷ tarum ilaṅku eṉṉum collaiyē mūlamākavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. itu mikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattaik kuṟikka vyākaraṇam eṉṉum collum, ilakkiyattaik kuṟikkac cākityam eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻilō ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷait tavira vēṟetuvumillai. aḵtōṭu, av viru coṟkaḷukkum vēr tamiḻiltāṉ uḷḷatu. aḵteṅṅaṉa meṉiṉ, kāṭṭuval:
eḻuccip poruḷai yuṇarttum ekara veḻuttai aṭiyākak koṇṭa el eṉṉuñ col, eḻukiṉṟa allatu utikkiṉṟa katiravaṉai yum ataṉ oḷiyaiyum kuṟikkum.
``ellē yilakkam'' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (iṭai 21)
el = katiravaṉ, veyil, oḷi, pakal.
el - eṉṟu = katiravaṉ.
eṉṟu - eṉṟavaṉ = katiravaṉ
eṉṟu - eṉṟūḻ = katiravaṉ mutircci, kōṭai.
ekaram allatu ēkāram eḻuccipporu ḷuṇarttuvatai, eḻu, empu, evvu, ekku, eṭu, ēṇ, ēṇi, ēṇai, ēttu, (ēṟṟiccol), ēntu, ēl, ēṟu, erect, edify, eructate mutaliya coṟkaḷiṟ kāṇka. uyarccipporuḷum eḻuccip poruḷō ṭokkum.
el - elku - elaku - ilaku = viḷaṅku.
coṟkaḷiṉ mōṉaittiripil ikaram ekaramākavum ekaram ikaramākavum tirivatu iyalpē.
ilaku - ilaṅku = viḷaṅku.
ilaṅku = ēri, kuḷam.
teḷinta nīr paḷiṅkupōl viḷaṅkutalāl, ēri ilaṅku eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
``mīṉka ṇaṟṟē ataṉcuṉai'' eṉṟu kapilar paṟampumalaic cuṉaiyai nilāviṟku oppiṭṭuk kūṟiyatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañci = ēri, kuḷam.
lañcu (lanj), raṅku (rang) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku ilaṅku eṉṉum teṉcollē mūlamākum.
ilaṅku - laṅku - raṅku (va.)
laṅku - lañcu - rañcu - rakta (va.)
ilaṅku - ilaṅkai = āṟṟiṭaikkuṟai, tīvu, irāvaṇaṉ āṇṭa nakaram allatu tīvu.
perunīriṭaippaṭṭa ciṟunilam taṉittu viḷaṅkuvatāl ilaṅkai eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
ilaṅku-ilakku = viḷakku, viḷakkam.
ilakkutal = colippittal, viḷaṅkavaittal, varaital, eḻututal, kuṟittal. ``irīrakai yilakkuka'' (caivaca. potu. 274).
varaṇantīṭṭip paṭam varaivatum eḻutteḻutuvatum viḷaṅkavaitta lākum. mutaṟkālattil paṭaveḻuttē (Linoglyph) vaḻakkiliruntatu. iṉṟum, eḻututal eṉṉuñ col, varaṇappaṭam varaitalaiyum eḻut teḻututalaiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṇṇukku maṟaintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai viḷakku viḷakkutalpōla, eḻuttum kaṇṇukku maṟaintuḷḷa karuttukaḷai viḷakkuvatākum. eḻututalum kuṟittalum oṉṟē.
ilakku - ilakki = eḻutu.
``ivvuruvu neñceṉṉuṅ kiḻiyiṉmē liruntilakkittu'' 	(cīvaka. 180)
ilakku allatu ilakki eṉṉun teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil lik eṉat tiriyum. itu mikamikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
lik=eḻutu, likita=eḻutappaṭṭatu, lipi=eḻuttu, lēkak= eḻuttāḷaṉ, lēka=eḻutappaṭṭatu, lēkaṉī=eḻutukōl-ivvāṟu ilakku eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil palavāṟākat tiricoṟkaḷai uṇṭu paṇṇiyuḷḷatu.
ilakku=kuṟipporuḷ, ampeyyuṅ kuṟi, aṭaiyāḷam, kuṟitta iṭam, kuṟitta camaiyam, kuṟitta allatu nāṭiya poruḷ, porakkuṟitta etiri, kuṟitta aḷavu.
ilakku vaittal = kuṟivaittal, ilakkuppārttal = kuṟipārttal, camaiyam pārttal - ivai iṉṟum tamiḻ vaḻakkām.
``ilakka muṭampiṭumpaik keṉṟu kalakkattaik
kaiyāṟāk koḷḷātām mēl''		(kuṟaḷ. 627)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oru kuṟitta iṭattai iṉṟum ilakku eṉpar nellai nāṭṭār. ilakku eṉṉum collē, ilattīṉil locus eṉṟu tirintu, āṅkilattil, local, locate, locative mutaliya coṟkaḷaip piṟappikkum. kuṟitta iṭam eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷait tarum tamiḻccol, ilattīṉil taṉ ciṟappupporuḷai yiḻantu, iṭam eṉṉum potupporuḷaiyē tarutal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē, oppōṉ kēṭṭalaik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum tamiḻccol, ilattīṉil kēṭṭalai maṭṭum kuṟikkum do eṉṉum ticaiccollākat tirintuḷḷatu.
eyvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyaṉṟi, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyum, ilakku eṉṟē collappaṭum.
``uṭaṉpiṟap paṉpiṟku ilakkāṉavar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ'' eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril, ilakkāṉavar eṉpatu eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉavar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ip poruḷil ilakku eṉṉum col ilakkiyam eṉṉum vaṭivam eytum. oru karuttai allatu kūṟṟai ilaṅka (viḷaṅka) vaikkum poruḷ ilakku eṉappaṭṭatu. āṅkilattiluḷḷa illustrate eṉṉum collum ip poruḷatē. L. illustro = to light up, to illuminate, il = in, lustro = to make light.
ilakku - ilakkam = oḷi, kuṟipporuḷ, eṇkuṟi, eṇṇiṭam, eṇ, pēreṇṇākiya nūṟāyiram.
ilakkamiṭutal = kaṇakkiṭutal.
mutalāvatu nūṟāyirattiṟkum nūṟu nūṟāyirattiṟkum tamiḻil taṉiccol illai. piṉṉar, pērilakkamākiya nūṟāyiram ilakka meṉṟum, kaṭaici yilakkamākiya nūṟu nūṟāyiram kōṭi yeṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
ilakkam - lakṣa (va.) - yh¡ (c.)- lac (E.)
kōṭi - kōṭṭi (va.) - kuṭōṭu (u.) -- crore (E.)
ilakku - ilakkar, ilakkāram = āṭai.
āṭai āḷkaḷai viḷaṅkacceyvatāl ilakkar eṉappaṭṭatu.
``āṭai yillātavaṉ araimaṉitaṉ''
ilakku - ilakkai = āṭai, campaḷam.
campaḷam oru kuṟiyetirppātalāl ilakkai yeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañciṉai - ilāñciṉai = kuṟi, aṭaiyāḷam, muttirai. ilañciṉai - ilacciṉai = kuṟi, muttirai. 
ilāñciṉai - lāñcaṉa (va.).
eḻuttaik kuṟikkum ilakku eṉṉum col, ākupeyarāyc collaiyum moḻiyaiyum uṇartti, `aṇam' vikuti peṟṟu moḻiyiṉ nūṟṟokutiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
Logos eṉṉum kirēkkaccol collaiyum, kaṭṭuraiyaiyum nūlaiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. ac collum ilakkam eṉṉum teṉ collukku iṉamāṉatē.
Gk. gramma = a letter; gramma - grammar (E.)
L. litera = a letter; litera - literature (E.)
iṅṅaṉam, āṅkilattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇa vilakkiyap peyarkaḷum, eḻuttaik kuṟikkuñ colliṉiṉṟē piṟantuḷḷamai kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilakkaṇam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉiṉṟē, lakṣaṇā eṉṉum vaṭacollum tirikkappaṭṭu, piṉpu tamiḻil ilakkaṇai eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
lakṣaṇā = kuṟippupporuḷ.
viṭṭa vilakkaṇai, viṭāta vilakkaṇai, viṭṭum viṭāta vilakkaṇai ākiya mūṉṟum kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavē. ilakkaṇaik kiṉamāṉa ākupeyar kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttu vaṉavaṟṟōṭu (pavaṇantiyārāl) cērkkap peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻil, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, vērp poruḷait tāṅki niṉṟu, eḻuttaiyuṅ kuṟittu, Grammar, literature eṉṉum poruḷait tarukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilō, avaṟṟukku nērāṉa lakṣaṇam lakṣyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vērccol aṟṟu, eḻuttaik kuṟikkumpōtu lik eṉa vēṟoru vaṭivam pūṇṭu, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyattaik kuṟikkātu kuṇaṅkuṟip poruḷaiyē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. iv vēṟupāṭṭaik kaṇṭukoḷka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum col liraṇṭum teṉcollē yeṉṟu teṟṟeṉat teḷika. kulap piriviṉai yāl tamiḻaṉukku vanta tāḻvu, viṭutalai yaṭainta piṉpum tamiḻait tākkātirukka.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum iraṇṭaiyuṅ kuṟikkat tamiḻil `aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam' eṉa vēṟiru coṟkaḷum uḷa. añcaṇaṅkam - aintilakkaṇam, añcaṇaṅkiyam - aintilakkiyam (yāḻppāṇa akarāti). aṇaṅkam - ilakkaṇam (potikai nikaṇṭu). aṇaṅkutal-olittal. ``pukaiyaṇaṅka'' (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai 10, 18). ivai ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akarātiyiṉ iṇaippu maṭalattilum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṇaṅku = oli, eḻuttu, aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkam = ilakkaṇam.
aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkiyam = ilakkiyam.
aṇaṅku eṉṉum viṉaiccol tūya taṉittamiḻc collātalil, aṇaṅkam, aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum peyarkaḷai vaṭacolleṉat talaiciṟanta tamiḻp pakaivarum vāytiṟavār eṉpatu teḷitarutēṟṟam.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' ēppiral 1948

tiruveṉṉum col teṉcollē
maruva larkoḷa maṉporu ḷaṟṟapaṉ
marapi ṉāruḷar maintilar vāyiru
tiruvi ḻantapiṉ ṟiruveṉuñ collaiyu
moruva niṉṟavaṉ tamiḻa ṉoruvaṉē!
mikamikac ciṟupāṉmaiyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paṉṉūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa teṉmoḻic coṟkaḷai vaṭamoḻiyil tirittukkoṇṭatu maṉṟi, collārāycciyum kalaimuṟai yaṟivum mikka iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilum avaṟṟai vaṭacoṟkaḷē yeṉṟu valippatum, vaṭacol vaṭivaṅ kaḷaiyē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅka neñcaḻuttaṅ koḷvatum vintaiyiṉum vintaiyē!
tiruveṉṉuñ col āriyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻccol leṉpataṟku, toṉmutu cārpu nūlāṉa tolkāppiyattilēyē atu āṭci peṟṟiruppatum mēlai yāriyamoḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum atu ev vaṭiviṉum kāṇappaṭāmaiyum, ataṉ vērpporuḷ vaṭamoḻi yilillātu tamiḻilēyē yiruppatum pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.
tiruveṉṉuñ col tamiḻil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. ciṟappu,     4. aḻaku, 5. kānti, 6. polivu, 7. pēṟu(pākkiyam), 8. tūymai allatu teyvattaṉmai, 9 nalviṉai, 10, kaṇiyaṉ(cōtiṭaṅ  kūṟuvōṉ), 11. māṅkaliyam, 12. paḻaiya talaiyaṇi vakai, 13. makaḷir koṅkaimēl tōṉṟum vīṟṟutteyvam, 14. tūymai allatu perumai kuṟikkum aṭaimoḻi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu; aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷil tirumai eṉap paṇpup peyar vikuti peṟṟum, celvam perumai tūymai mutaliya poruḷkaḷil tiruvam eṉa ammīṟu peṟṟum, ak kuṇaṅkaḷaik kuṟittut tiruvaṉ, tiruvāḷaṉ, tiruvāṭṭi, tiruvattavar, tiruviṉāḷ eṉa uyartiṇaip peyarākiyum vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu. srī eṉṉum ataṉ vaṭacol vaṭivamō kaṭaikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟkālattatāy, atuvum urainūlkaḷil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. pēṟu, 4. aḻaku, 5. tūymai yaṭaimoḻi eṉṉum aimporuḷkaḷilē vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṭaṅkiviṭa, avarālum moḻi nūlaṟivum tāymoḻip  paṟṟum aṟṟa tamiḻarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu. srī eṉpatiluḷḷa cakaram tamiḻc cakarattai yotta oliyiṉatā yiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tiruveṉṉuñ collukku mēṟkūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷuḷ  celvam, aḻaku, tūymai yeṉṉu mūṉṟē mukkiyamum piṟavaṟṟukku aṭippaṭaiyumākum. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, celvam eṉpatē mutalatākum.
celvattiṉāl oruvar ciṟakkavuṇṭu iṉpamāy vāḻvatāl   uṭal moḻumoḻuveṉa aḻaku peṟukiṉṟatu; vaṟumaiyiṉāl vayiṟu kāyntu maṉaṅkavaṉṟu veyilil vāṭi mēṉi keṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉālēyē, celvamuḷḷa iṉattārai veḷḷokka leṉṟum vaṟumaiyuṟṟa iṉat tāraik kārokka leṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar nam muṉṉōr. celva mikutiyiṉāl oruvarkkup perumaiyum teyvattaṉmaiyum uṇṭākiṉṟatu; ulakiṟ celvamikka aracaṉ teyvat taṉmaiyuṭaiyavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaṭavuḷ ulakelāmuṭaiya celvar. aḷaviṟanta matippē teyva vaṇakkamākum. matippuc celvattāl varuvatu. aṟivum oruvakaic celvamē. teyvattaṉmaiyuḷḷa viṭamellām tūymai karutappaṭum.
āḻntu nōkkiṉ, tiruveṉṉuñ collukkuriya poruḷ kaḷellām mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; nōkkik kāṇka. 
tiruveṉṉuñ col ippōtuḷḷapaṭi mutaṉmutalākat tol kāppiyattil, 
piṟappē kuṭimai āṇmai āṇṭō
ṭuruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil
niṟaiyē aruḷē uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa
muṟaiyaṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakaiyē	(tol. meyp. 25)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, celvam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. piṉṉart tirukkuṟaḷil,
aḻukkā ṟeṉavoru pāvi tirucceṟṟut
tīyuḻi yuyttu viṭum,	(kuṟaḷ. 168)

ūruṇi nīrniṟain taṟṟē yulakavām

iṉattāṟṟi yeṇṇāta vēntaṉ ciṉattāṟṟic
cīṟiṟ ciṟukun tiru,	(kuṟaḷ. 568)
muyaṟci tiruviṉai yākku muyaṟṟiṉmai

irumaṉap peṇṭiruṅ kaḷḷuṅ kavaṟun

karumaṇiyiṟ pāvāynī pōtāyām vīḻun
tirunutaṟ killai yiṭam	(kuṟaḷ. 1123)
eṉac celvam, (celvattiṟkut talaiviyāṉa) tirumakaḷ, aḻaku  eṉṉum mupporuḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu; piṟa poruḷkaḷil piṟa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu.
iṉi, iruvakai vaḻakkilum, tēviyal makaviyal eṉṉum iru vakaic cārpil, tiru eṉṉum col tūymai, celvam, aḻaku, maṅkalam, kaṇṇiyam eṉṉum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil aṭai moḻiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvataip piṉvaruṅ  kāṭṭukaḷā laṟika.
1. tēviyal
(1) teyvam: tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumāl, tirumaṭantai.
nūl:	tirukkaṭaikkāppu, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirukkuṟuntāṇṭakam,   tirukkōvai, tiruneṭuntāṇṭakam, tiruppatikam, tiruppallāṇṭu, tiruppāṭṭu, tiruppāvai, tiruppukaḻ, tirumantiram, tirumuṟai, tirumoḻi, tiruvakuppu, tiruvācakam, tiruvāymoḻi,  tiruveḻuttu.
ūṇ:	tirukkaṉṉalamutu, tiruppaṭimāṟṟu, tirumaturam, tirumālai vaṭai, tiruvamutu.
aṇi:	tiruccuṇṇam, tirunīṟu, tiruppaṭṭam, tiruppāvāṭai, tirumaṇ, tiruvātti.
poruḷ:	tirucciṉṉam, tirumaram, tirumalar, tirumuṭṭu, tirumuḷaippālikai, tiruvalaku, tiruvācikai, tiruvāṭutaṇṭu, tiruviḷakku.
iṭam:	tiruccentūr, tirunakar, tirunāṭu, tirunelvēli,  tiruppati, tirumalai. tiruvaraṅkam, tiruvitāṅkūr, tiruvēṅkaṭam, tirukkaṇ, tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭi, tirukkaṟṟaḷi, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruppāṟkaṭal, tirumaṭam, tirumāḷikai, tirumuṟṟam, tirumuṉ,  tiruvuṇṇāḻikai, tiruvōlakkam.
kālam:  tirunāḷ, tiruvātirai, tiruvōṇam.

kuṇam:	tirukkuṟippu, tiruvaruḷ, tirukkōlam.
toḻil:	tirukkaliyāṇam, tirukkārttikai, tiruviḻā, tiruvūṟal, tirukkāppu, tirukkūttu, tiruttoṇṭu, tiruppaṇi,  tiruppaḷḷi, tirumuḻukku, tiruviḷaiyāṭal.
(2) aṭiyār:	tirukkūṭṭam, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruttakkatēvar,  tiruttoṇṭar, tirumūlar, tiruvaṭimār, tiruvaḷḷuvar, tiruvōṭu.
2. makaviyal
(1) vēttiyal: tirumaṟu, tirumukam, tirumuttu, tirumūppu, tirumantiram, tiruvāṇai, tiruvāykkēḷvi, tiruvōlai.
(2) potuviyal: tirunilaikkāl, tirunilaimakaḷir, tiruppūṭṭu, tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumaṅkaliyam, tirumaṇam, tiruvāṅkutal, tiruvil, tiruviṉai, tiruvuḻuttu(aṇi).
ivaṟṟuḷ, tirunelvēli, tiruvaraṅkam mutaliya iṭappeyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu ulakavaḻakkil tiruveṉṉum aṭaipeṟṟē vaḻaṅku vataiyum, srīraṅkam eṉṉum vaṭivam iruvakai vaḻakkilum piṟkālatta tātalaiyum nōkkuka. itaṉāl, tiruvilliputtūr eṉpatē srī villiputtūr eṉpataṉ paṇṭai vaṭivamum eṉa aṟika.
tirumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar tirumāṉ eṉa maruvic celvam allatu  perumai kuṟikkum aṭaimoḻiyākum. perumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar perumāṉ eṉa maruvutal kāṇka. tiru eṉpatai srī eṉṟu tirittataṉāl, tirumāṉ eṉṉum aṭaiyai srīmāṉ eṉṟu tirippar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ataṉuḷ, māṉ eṉṉum īṟṟai mat eṉa maṟṟum tirittu, srīmat, srīmati eṉpavaṟṟai muṟaiyē āṇpāl  peṇpā laṭaimoḻikaḷāka muḻu vaṭavaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvar. tirumakaṉ, tirumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkaṭkut tirumakaḷ, tirumāṭṭi eṉpavai tamiḻil muṟaiyē peṇ pālākum. perumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkup perumāṭṭi eṉpatu peṇpālātal kāṇka. srī eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivait tamiḻil cī eṉac citaittut tirumāṉ tirumāṭṭi eṉpavaṟṟaic cīmāṉ cīmāṭṭiyeṉa eḻutuvatu, kōḻikkōṭu eṉpatai Calicut eṉṉum āṅkilavaṭivu vāyilāyk kaḷḷikkōṭṭai yeṉṟeḻutuvatum, kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉpatai Calcutta eṉṉum āṅkila vaṭivu vāyilāyk kalkattā eṉṟeḻutuvatum pōṉṟa aṟiyāmaiyēyākum. itaṉāl, cīkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭiviṉum tirukkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭivē takkatākum eṉa aṟika.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉuñ collaiyaṉṟic ciṟuveṉṉuñ collaiyum srī eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. peṇṭirkkuc contamākavuḷḷa ciṟucelvattiṟku allatu celvattiṟkuc `ciṟupāṭu' eṉṟum ciṟutaṉam eṉṟum peyar.
ciṟutaṉan tēṭuvaḷ 		(taṇṭalai. 95)
vīṭṭuc celavil mīttuvaikkum ciṟu tokaikkuc cilvāṉam eṉṟu peyar. ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu piṟkālattil taṉi vēṟṟu (private) niti eṉap potuppaṭavum poruḷ peṟṟatu.
uṭaiyārsrī rājarāja tēvar ciṟutaṉattuk
koṭutta (S. I. I. ii, 3)
ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu srītaṉam, cītaṉam eṉat tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
srī eṉpatait tiruveṉṟum, srīlasrī eṉpatait tirupperuntiru allatu tirumikutiru eṉṟum, makārājarājasrī eṉpatai makavaraca varacattiru allatu māvaraca varacattiru eṉṟum, rājasrī eṉpatai aracattiru eṉṟum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum. ma. (rā). (rā). srī eṉṉum mukavariyaṭaiyil, srī eṉpatu tavira maṟṟa mūveḻuttukaḷum tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷāyē yiruttalaik kāṇka. itaṉāl, srī eṉpatu piṉṉai vaṭiva meṉpatai yōrka.
cīrkāḻi eṉpataṉ mutal vaṭivam cīkāḻi eṉpar. cīr eṉṉuñ col centamiḻāyiruttaliṉ, cīrkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkuvatu nallatu. itaṟ kicaiyātār tirukkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal  vēṇṭum.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉun tamiḻccoṟku vērpporuḷ eṉṉaiyō veṉiṟ kūṟuvēṉ:
tiruveṉpatu til eṉṉum vēraṭiyāyp piṟantu tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu allatu īṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.

`kālam' eṉṉuñ col emmoḻikkuriyatu?
vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvākavō, av viraṇṭa ṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkē ciṟappākavō uriya pala coṟkaḷpaṟṟi av viru moḻi yāḷarkkum toṉṟutoṭṭu nikaḻntuvarum pōr kaṟṟār aṉaivarkkum terintatē. ac coṟkaḷuḷ  ‘fhy«’ eṉpatum oṉṟākum.
oru collai oru moḻikkuriyatu eṉṟu kūṟutaṟku oppiyaṉ bkhÊüš(Comparative Philology) aṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭap paṭum. intiyāvil, ciṟappāyt tamiḻnāṭṭil, moḻinūl eṉṉum peyaraiyē kēṭṭaṟiyātāruṅkūṭa, vaṭamoḻippaṟṟoṉṟē kāraṇamākap pala teṉcoṟkaḷai  vaṭacoṟkaḷākak kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. avaruḷḷum āṅkilak   kalvippaṭṭam peṟṟavarō veṉṟāl, tammai ellā moḻi kaḷilum vallunareṉṟē eṇṇikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. moḻinūl eṉpatu potuk kalviyiṉiṉṟum, allatu ilakkiya vilakkaṇak kalviyiṉiṉṟum vēṟāṉa oru taṉikkalai eṉpatai avar caṟṟum uṇarntilar.
potuvāka, vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉarellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilum akarātikaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷē eṉṟu eṇṇikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. ulakattiluḷḷa palvēṟu moḻikaḷum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟupāṭuṭaiyavēṉum, oru marattiṉ palvēṟu kiḷaikaḷ ōr aṭimarattiṉiṉṟē kiḷaittuḷḷatu pōla, oru mūlamoḻiyiṉiṉṟē kiḷaittuḷḷaṉavākum. cila coṟkaḷilum, ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟaikaḷilum ulaka moḻikaḷellām ottiruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. ulakattiluḷḷa palvēṟu makkaṭkulaṅkaḷum orutāy vayiṟṟiṉa eṉpatu iṟṟai moḻiyārāycciyāl teriyavarutaliṉ, avaṟṟāl pēcappaṭum palvēṟu moḻikaḷum oru mūlattiṉa eṉṟē koḷḷattakum.
aiyāyiram mailukkappāṟpaṭṭa ilattīṉ, kirēkka moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkum potuvāṉa cila coṟkaḷuḷa. 
e-ṭu: navis(L.) = nāvāy.  palios(Gk.) = paḻaiya.
iru kulattār allatu nāṭṭār kalantu allatu aṭuttaṭuttu  vāḻiṉ, cila poruḷkaḷait tamakkuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum māṟṟikkoḷvatupōlac cila coṟkaḷaiyuṅ māṟṟikkoḷvar. utāraṇamāka, āṅkilarukkum tamiḻarukkum toṭarpuṇṭāṉapiṉ Father(ghâÇ), Battalion(g£lhs«) mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ tamiḻilum, g©o(bandy), njh¥ò(tope)  mutaliya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āṅkilattilumākak kalantuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyum teṉmoḻiyum aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉamaiyiṉ, ēṟkeṉavē iv viraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa potuc coṟkaḷ nīṅkalākap putitāka oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ ettuṇaiyō palavām. vaṭamoḻiyilum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ allatu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ uṇṭeṉpataik kālañceṉṟa kālṭuvel, kuṇṭarṭ eṉṉum iruperu moḻinūlārāycciyāḷar eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.   ivaruḷ muṉṉaiyavar vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum teṉcoṟkaḷaiyum  pirittaṟivataṟkum vitikaḷ vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavai iraṇṭu. avaiyāvaṉa:
1. oru col enta moḻiyil palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉum iṉaccoṟkaḷuṭaṉum mūlapporuḷait tāṅkikkoṇṭu peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatō, anta moḻikkē aḵtu uriyatu eṉpatu.
2. vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉmoḻiyilum vaḻaṅkum oru col vaṭamoḻiyiṉ iṉamāṉa mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷ oṉṟiluṅ kāṇappaṭāmal, tamiḻiṉ iṉamāṉa maṟṟellāt tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluṅ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ atu tamiḻukkē uriya collām eṉpatu.
vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum, piṟa moḻikaḷellām taṉṉiṭattiṟ kaṭaṉkoḷḷat tāṉmaṭṭum oru collum piṟa moḻi yiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷātu taṉittiyaṅkum tūymai vāynta teṉṟum cila vaṭamoḻivāṇar poyyum pulaiyum malinta purāṇak kālattaṉṟi, ārāyccimikka ikkālattum pitaṟṟuvatu, mikavum eḷḷinakaiyāṭat takkataṉṟi vēṟu eṉ ceyaṟpālatu?
vaṭamoḻi ikkālat tamiḻiṟ perituṅ kalantiruppatāl ēṟkeṉavē iv viraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa potuc coṟkaḷōṭu, putitāy vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟa tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷaiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷē yeṉṟu naṭunilai ārāycciyillā vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉarellām nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟai yaṟaikiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilac coṟkaḷ nūṟṟukku muppatu vītam iṟṟait tamiḻil teruppēcciṟ kalantiruppiṉum, nūlvaḻakkiṟ kalantilatu. aṅṅaṉam orukāl nūlvaḻakkilum kalakkumāyiṉ, piṉṉar ārāycciyillātār all, beetle, crack, derive, ear, fear, galley.  bail, in, jury, kill, locality, mess, near, one, put, quoth, roll, shrink, through, un, vain. want, yean, zenith eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟkaḷē   muṟaiyē ellām, viṭṭil, kiṟukku, tiri, ēr, veru, kalam, āli, il, cūḷ, kol, ilakku, micai, neruṅku, oṉṟu,  pōṭu, kūṟu, uruḷ, curuṅku, turuva, al, vīṇ, vēṇṭu, īṉ,  ceṉṉi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷākat tirintuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvaraṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ tiripākap pala tūya teṉcoṟkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉareṉka.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvōr tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattaiyum oruciṟitum nōkkukiṉṟilar. uṇmai eṉṉum kuṇam niṉaivu, col, ceyal eṉṟa mūṉṟaiyum taḻuviyatu eṉpatai uṇarttaṟkut tamiḻil uṇmai
(uḷ = maṉam), vāymai(vāy), meymmai   (bkŒ = uṭampu) eṉa mūvēṟu coṟkaḷ uḷa. marattiṉ uṟuppukaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum ataṉataṉ vēṟupāṭṭiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu peyar uḷatu. ilaiyeṉṉum orē yuṟuppiṟku ilai, tāḷ, tōkai, ōlai eṉa nāṉku coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ovvoru collum ovvoru taṉmaiyaik kuṟikkum. pūviṉ vevvēṟu nilaikaḷaik kuṟittaṟku vevvēṟu coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalāvatu tōṉṟumpōtu arumpu; pērarumpāyiṉ pōtu; malarntapiṉ  malar; viḻuntapiṉ vī ; vāṭiyapiṉ cemmal. uḷḷattiṉ vevvēṟu toḻiṟkum vevvēṟu col uḷa. e-ṭu: uḷ(ḷu), to will; uṇar to feel, to comprehend; niṉai, to think, to remember; niṉaivukūr, to remember, to commemorate; uṉ(ṉu), to imagine, (c‹d« = imagination); eṇ(ṇu), to deliberate, to consider; karutu, to conceive (fU¤J = concept, idea); aṟi, to know, koḷ, to opine, (nfhŸ = opinion, koḷkai = doctrine); mati, to estimate, to regard; muṉ(ṉu), to propose; muṉṉiṭu, to propose, to set before, (K‹Å£L = having proposed, having set before, for the purpose of); tīrmāṉi, to determine, to resolve; naya, to appreciate; teruḷ, to perceive clearly. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻiṟ pala poruḷum paṟṟip pala coṟkaḷiruppavum ivaṟṟaik kavaṉiyātum, ivaṟṟiṉ nuṭpavēṟupāṭukaḷai ārāyātum, ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkiyum, tamiḻiṟ poruḷuṇarttap pōtiya collillai yeṉṟu paḻittut tirivar.
ivaṟṟuḷ, mati yeṉṉuñ col ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa mūlamoḻic collākum. am mūlamoḻikkup piṟa moḻikaḷiṉum tamiḻ mika neruṅkiyateṉpatu moḻinūl ārāycciyāl viḷaṅkum. itupaṟṟik kālṭuvel aiyarum piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. (evarum eṅṅaṉamum aiyuṟāmaip poruṭṭu avar kūṟṟiṉ mūlattaiyē moḻipeyarkkātu)  iṅkut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
“Nevertheless, I regard the comparison of words, when carefully and cautiously conducted, as an important help to the determination of lingual affinities, and it will be found, I think, that the following vocabularies bear independent testimony, in their own degree, to the same result at which we arrived by grammatical comparison viz. that the Dravidian idioms exhibit traces of an ancient, deep-seated connection with pre-Sanskrit—the assumed archaic mother-tongue of the Indo-European family, whilst at the same time the traces they exhibit of relationship to the languages  of the Scythian group, especially to the Ugrian tongues, are on the whole, closer, more distinctive, and more essential.”
- (Comparative Grammar of the Dravidian Languages, p. 452).
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē kālattaik kuṟippataṟkum tamiḻiṟ pala coṟkaḷuḷa. avai kāl, poḻutu, vēḷai, koṉ, cevvi, aṟṟam, ēlvai, camaiyam, nēram, ñāṉṟu, patam mutaliyaṉavām.
ivaṟṟuḷ kāl eṉpatu kālai, kālam eṉat tirintu neṭuṅ kālattaiyum kuṟuṅkālattaiyum kuṟikkum. ēṉaiyavellām kuṟuṅ kālattaiyē kuṟikkum. poḻutu eṉpatu pōḻtu, pōtu eṉat tiriyum. perumpoḻutu eṉpatu iru mātaṅkoṇṭa gUt¤ijí«(Season) ciṟupoḻutu eṉpatu nāṉmaṇi nēraṅkoṇṭa nāṭpakutiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vēḷai eṉpatu nāṭpakutiyaik kuṟikkum; ceyyuḷil vēlai eṉṟum tiriyum.  koṉ eṉpatu uriya tīya kālattaiyum, cevvi eṉpatu takunta   naṟcamaiyattaiyum, aṟṟam eṉpatu takunta tīccamaiyattaiyum kuṟikkum. ēlvai eṉpatu elvaiyeṉṟuṅ kuṟukum. camaiyam eṉpatu amaiyameṉṟum tirintu, takunta vēḷaiyai allatu paruvamāṉa nilaiyaik kuṟikkum. nēram eṉpatu oru kuṟitta nērattaiyum oru viṉai nikaḻccikkuriya fhy¤ijí«(duration) kuṟikkum. patam eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ cevvaiyāṉa nilaiyaik kuṟikkum. ñāṉṟu eṉpatu pōtu eṉpataip pōṉṟatu.
kāl, kālai, poḻutu, vēḷai, ēlvai, camaiyam, nēram, ñāṉṟu, patam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ. vantakāl, vantakālai, vanta poḻutu, vantavēḷai eṉa oru kuṟitta nērattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
oru nāḷiṉ oḷi iruḷ vēḷaikaḷaik kuṟippataṟkup pakal iravu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum, oru nāḷiṟ kālai 6 maṇi mutal naṉṉāṉku maṇi nēramākavuḷḷa aṟuvakaic ciṟupoḻutukaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippataṟkuk kālai, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu, mālai, yāmam, vaikaṟai eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. vēlaik kālattaik kaṇippataṟku 24 nimiṭa nēraṅkoṇṭa nāḻikai eṉṉum aḷavuḷḷatu.
oru vārattiṉ ēḻu  nāḷkaḷum ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy,  aṟivaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, kāri eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ aṟivaṉ, kāri eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum patilākap putaṉ, caṉi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. vāram, kiḻamai eṉpaṉa urimai eṉṉum oruporuṭ   coṟkaḷ. (kāri = kariyatu. kariyavaṉ eṉṟu cilappatikārattuḷḷum(10 : 102), `maimmīṉ' eṉṟu  puṟanāṉūṟṟuḷḷum(117) vantiruttal kāṇka. mai = kaṟuppu.)
iruvāram cērntatu  oru pakkam eṉappaṭum. itaiyē pakṣam eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyār. (āṭit tiṅkaṭ pēriruṭ pakkattu cilap. 23 : 133. pakkam = pakuti; iruṭpakkam = kiruṣṇa pakṣam.)
irupakkam cērntatu oru tiṅkaḷ (mātam).
iru tiṅkaḷ cērntatu oru perumpoḻutu. cittirai mutal paṅkuṉi yīṟāka, ivviru mātamāy, muṟaiyē, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉa āṟu perumpoḻutukaḷākum. ivai mikap paḻaiya ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyattiṟ collappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē poruntuvaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. ākaiyāl, ivaṟṟukku  vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kūṟum peyarkaḷellām moḻipeyarppē.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum ippōtu vaḻaṅkivarum peyarkaḷ vaṭa coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟukkum patilākap paṉṉīr irācippeyarkaḷē malaiyāḷa nāṭṭiṟpōl paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭilum vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. paṉṉīr irācip peyarkaḷil mēṭam(mēḻam),  iṭapam, kaṭakam, kaṉṉi, tulām, kumpam, mīṉam eṉṉum ēḻum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ allatu tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻik kum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalāṟukkum muṟaiyē takar, kuṇṭai, alavaṉ, maṭantai, tūkku, kuṭam eṉṟum peyarkaḷuṇṭu. ēṉaiya mituṉam, ciṅkam, viruccikam, taṉucu, makaram eṉṉum aintum tamiḻil muṟaiyē iraṭṭai, āḷi, tēḷ, vil, cuṟā eṉappaṭum. irācikkut tamiḻil ōrai eṉṟu peyar.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaḷum irācip peyar peṟumāṟu:
 1. 	cittirai	-	mēṭam	 7.	aippaci	-	tulām	 
 2. 	vaikāci	-	iṭapam	 8. 	kārttikai	-	viruccikam
 3. 	āṉi	-	mituṉam	 9. 	mārkaḻi	-	taṉucu
 4. 	āṭi	-	kaṭakam	10. 	tai	-	makaram
 5. 	āvaṇi	-	ciṅkam	11. 	māci	-	kumpam
 6. 	puraṭṭāci	-	kaṉṉi	12. 	paṅkuṉi	-	mīṉam
acuvaṉi mutal rēvati yīṟāṉa irupattēḻu naṭcattiraṅkaṭkum tamiḻiṟ peyaruṇṭu. avai muṟaiyē puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟalai, mūtirai, kaḻai, kāṟkuḷam, kaṭcevi, koṭunukam, kaṇai, uttaram, kai, aṟuvai, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉai, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, uṭaikuḷam, kaṭaikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākkai, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟai nikaṇṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
āṇṭu, muyaṟci, col, nañcu, makiḻcci, nōy, viruppam, ūtiyam, iḻappu, varuttam, nilā, āciriyaṉ, kōyil, nīr, muḻukku, kuḷippu, cōṟu, tēr, tiruviḻā, paṟavai, vilaṅku, mēlāṭai, illampukal, cāppāṭṭuk kaṭai, valamvaral, cilampu kaḻittal allatu, kāppuk kaḻaṟṟal, nīṭuvāḻi, mūtta makaṉ, tammaracu, irumaiyum ottu eṉṉum centamiḻc coṟ kaḷukkum coṟṟoṭarkaḷukkum muṟaiyē, varuṣam, pirayācai, vārttai, viṣam, cantōṣam, viyāti, iṣṭam, lāpam, naṣṭam, kaṣṭam, cantiraṉ, upāttiyāyar, ālayam, jalam, apiṣēkam, snāṉam, cātam, ratam, uṟcavam, pakṣi, mirukam, aṅkavastiram, kirukappiravēcam, pōjaṉacālai, piratakṣiṇam (ceytal), kaṅkaṇa visarjaṉam, cirañcīvi, jēṣṭa kumāraṉ, cuyarājyam, upayāṉu cammatamāy eṉṉum vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum paṉṉūṟṟuk kaṇakkiṉa. ivai pōṉṟavaiyē paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum irupattēḻu naṭcattiraṅkaṭkum itupōtu  vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷum eṉka.
 naṭcattiram tamiḻil nāḷ allatu nāṇmīṉ eṉappaṭum. potuvāṉatāyiṉ mīṉ eṉṟum uṭu eṉṟum veḷḷi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oru pakalum ōr iravuñ cērntu oru kiḻamai. oru kiḻamaikkuriya naṭcattiram nāḷ.nāḷum kiḻamaiyum nalintōrk killai eṉṟār auvaiyār. āyiṉum, nāḷ eṉpatu kiḻamai yeṉṉum poruḷilēyē iruvakai vaḻakkilum perumpālum vaḻaṅkivarum. kirakam kōḷ allatu kōṇmīṉ eṉappaṭum.
aṟupatāṇṭiṟ korumuṟaiyāka nikaḻnta cila pañcaṅkaḷpaṟṟi, allatu makkaḷiṉ carācari vāḻnāḷpaṟṟi, aṟupatāṇṭu eṉṉum aḷavu paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭil cila mutiyōrkkuḷ peyaraḷavāy maṭṭum vaḻaṅki vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. atu naṭaimuṟaiyiṟ kaṇikkappaṭavillai. cālivākaṉaṉālēyē aṟupatāṇṭiṟkum peyariṭappaṭṭatākat teri kiṉṟatu. avai yāvum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. avaṟṟukkut teṉcol illai. aṟupatāṇṭuc cakkaram cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varutaliṉ, atu āṅkila āṇṭu  pōlac  carittirattiṟkum neṭuṅkālak kuṟippiṟkum payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟu. paṇṭait tamiḻar avvav varacariṉ āṭcik kālattaikkoṇṭē ōr āṇṭaiyō neṭuṅkālattaiyō kuṟittum kaṇakkiṭṭum vantatākak kalveṭṭiṉiṉṟum teriyavarukiṉṟatu. (e-ṭu: kōpparakēcari māṟa varmaṉukku 24ām āṇṭu). oru neṭuṅkālattaik kaṇakkiṭuvataṟkup pala aracarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattaik kūṭṭavēṇṭiyatirukkum. ēṉeṉil aṭikkaṭi aracar māṟuvatu vaḻakkam. (carittirakālattil) ōr  aracaṉ evvaḷavu nīṭittāḷiṉum nūṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭuvatu mikavum arumai. ik kuṟaiyai nīkkutaṟku oru periyār kālattiṉiṉṟu, allatu oru nakarat tōṟṟattiṉiṉṟu, allatu oru peru nikaḻcciyiṉiṉṟu neṭuṅkālattaik kaṇakkiṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟukku muṟaiyē meykaṇṭāṉ yāṇṭum, kollam āṇṭum, kaliyāṇṭum utāraṇamākum. āṅkila mukammatiya āṇṭukaḷum periyār kālattiṉiṉṟum  kaṇakkiṭap paṭuvaṉavē.
aṟupatāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ūḻi eṉṉum aḷavu, paṇṭait tamiḻ nāṭṭil nikaḻnta cila kaṭalkōḷkaḷpaṟṟi, oru varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi īraḻiviṟ kiṭaippaṭṭa allatu oru viri vaḷarcciyai (Evolution)  kuṟikkiṉṟa oru neṭuṅkālattaik kuṟikka allatu kaṇikka vaḻaṅkivantatu. itu iv vāciriyar(tolkāppiyar) ātiyūḻiyiṉ  antattē in nūl (tol kāppiyam) ceytaliṉ eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tolkāppiyak kaṟpiyal  4ām cūttira vuraiyiṟ kūṟutalāṉum, aimpūtaṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟum ovvōr  ūḻiyil tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu paripāṭal (2 : 3-19) kūṟutalāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ākaiyāl itupōtu pañcāṅkattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa yuka aḷavu tamiḻar ūḻikkup poruntuvataṉṟu;  tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭalkōḷ kaḷiṉ iṭaikkālaṅkaḷ avvak kālattiṟ kaṇikkappaṭṭirukkalām. āṉāl, ataippaṟṟiya oru kuṟippum namakkuk kiṭaittilatu. iṟṟaik kaliyūḻi talaiccaṅkat tiṟutiyil nikaḻnta kaṭal kōḷiṉiṉṟum kaṇikkappaṭṭatāyiṉ, kaliyukam toṭaṅki 5041 āṇṭukaḷ āyiṉa eṉṉum kaṇakkup poruntuvatākum.
 mēṟkūṟiya kālap peyarkaḷuḷ kālam eṉṉuñ collaiyē ciṟappākak kūṟa eḻunta tik kaṭṭuraiyātaliṉ, ataṉ mūlattaiyum atu emmoḻiyaic cērntateṉpataiyum iṉi ārāyvōm.
kālam eṉṉum col, kāl eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. itu kāl eṉuñ col taṉittum, orukāl, vantakkāl eṉat toṭar moḻikaḷilum vantu kālapporuḷ taruvatāl   aṟiyappaṭum. tūṇ eṉpatu am īṟu peṟṟut tūṇam eṉṟu āṉāṟpōlak kāl eṉpatum am īṟu peṟṟuk kālam eṉṟāyatu. ai īṟupeṟiṉ kālai eṉṟākum; pār (kampi) eṉpatu pārai eṉṟāṉāṟpōla.
kāl, kālai, kālam eṉṉum mūṉṟu coṟkaḷum tamiḻil iruvakai vaḻakkilum mikap peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
kāl eṉpatu orukāl mukkālum eṉṉum toṭarkaḷilum, vantakkāl pōṉakkāl eṉa etirkāla viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷilum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iv viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷai vantākkā pōṉākkā eṉṟu citaittu vaḻaṅkuvar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭamēṟpakutiyi luḷḷavar.
piṉmuṉ kālkaṭai vaḻiiṭat teṉṉum
aṉṉa marapiṟ kālaṅ kaṇṇiya
eṉṉa kiḷaviyum avaṟṟiyal piṉavē	(tol. col. 714)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattil, kāl eṉpatu oru viṉaiyecca vikutiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyam evvitattum iṟṟaikku 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatākum.
kālai eṉpatu oru nāḷiṉ ciṟanta kālai allatu vēḷaiyākiya viṭiyaṟkālattai uṇarttum. kālai, viṭikālai eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku. kālaiyum mālaiyum kaikūppik kāltoḻutāl eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum ac col ap poruḷil vantatu. kālai eṉṉum col muṟpakalaiyum, muṟpakaliṉ   muṟpakutiyaiyum kuṟippatumuṇṭu.
iruvīṟṟum urittē cuṭṭuṅ kālai
icaiyuṭa ṉarukum teriyuṅ kālai
eṉat tolkāppiyattil, kālai eṉṉuñ col poḻutu, vēḷai eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷpōla oru kuṟitta nērattai uṇarttiṟṟu.
kālam eṉṉum col oru kuṟitta kālattaiyum viṭiyaṟ  kālattaiyum paruva kālattaiyum oru kālap pakutiyaiyum potuvāṉa kālattaiyum uṇarttum. viṭiyaṟkālam, cāyaṅkālam irākkālam, pēṟukālam, tuṉpakālam, kālattiṉāṟ ceyta naṉṟi(kuṟaḷ. 102), āyiyal nilaiyuṅ kālat tāṉum(tolkāppiyam) eṉṉun toṭar kaḷil oru kuṟitta kālamum kālattālē yeḻuntu (kāttālai yeḻuntu eṉpatu koccai vaḻakku.) eṉṉun toṭaril viṭiyaṟkālamum; vēṉiṟ kālam(kōṭaikālam), maḻaikālam(kārkālam) mutaliya toṭarkaḷil paruvakālamum; muṟkālam, taṟkālam, kaṟkālam, kīḻōrk kākiya kālam(tol.) mutaliya toṭarkaḷil oru kālappakutiyum poruḷiṭam kālam ciṉaikuṇam toḻil eṉṉun toṭaril potuvāṉa kālamum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āṅkilattil `Tense' eṉṟu kūṟum viṉaiyilakkaṇattaik kuṟikkat tamiḻil kālam eṉṉum collē koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
viṉaiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumai koḷḷātu
niṉaiyuṅ kālaik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum	(tol. col. 683)
kālan tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa	(tol. col. 684)
iṟappiṉ nikaḻviṉ etirviṉ eṉṟā
ammuk kālamum kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷum	(tol. col. 685)
viṉaiccol kālaṅkāṭṭuvatāl kālakkiḷavi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. kiḷavi-col.
māraik kiḷaviyum pallōr paṭarkkaik
kālak kiḷaviyoṭu muṭiyum eṉpa 	(tol. col. viṉai. 10)
tamiḻc ceyyuḷuṟuppukaḷ irupattunāṉkaṉuḷ kālamum oṉṟākat tolkāppiyattuṭ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kēṭpōr kaḷaṉē kālavakaieṉāa...	 (tol. poruḷ. cey. 1)
aḵṟiṇai muṭipupeṟum uyartiṇaic coṟkaḷuḷ kālam eṉpatum oṉṟākak kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar.
kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē
.....................................
 pālpirin ticaiyā uyartiṇai mēṉa	(tol. col. kiḷavi. 58)
 kālam eṉpatu vaṭacollāyiṉ, tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟappukunta tolkāppiyattil ataṉaik kūṟār āciriyar.
kāl eṉṉuñ coṟkut tamiḻiṟ pala poruḷkaḷiruppiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ nāṉkē talaimaiyāṉavum, piṟavaṟṟiṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉavu mākum. avai fhš(leg), kālvāy, kāṟṟu  (kāl + tu), kālam eṉpaṉa vākum, in nāṉkaṉuḷḷum, kūrntu nōkkiṉ, mutalatē ēṉai mūṉṟiṟkum mūlamākum.
oru coṟkup pala poruḷiruppiṉ avai yāvum orē toṭar puṭaiyaṉavā yirukkum. toṭarpiṉṟēl orē vaṭivuḷḷa vevvēṟu coṟkuriyaṉavā yirukkum. iṅkuk kāl eṉṉuñ coṟkuk kūṟiya nāṉku poruḷum orē toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavē, eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, nīṇṭatu eṉṉum karuttaiyē avai nāṉkum taḻuviyuḷḷaṉa. kāl eṉṉum uṟuppu (oṭuṅki) nīṇṭiruppatu; kālvāy nīṇṭu ōṭuvatu; kāṟṟu ōriṭattu nillātu ticai neṭuka nīṇṭu vīcuvatu; kālam oru muṭiviṉṟi nīṇṭu celvatu.  in nīṭcik karuttuk kāl eṉṉum uṟuppiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiya tākum.
kāl eṉṉum uṟuppiṉ peyar kāl eṉṉum viṉaiyiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyatākum. kālutal cintutal, allatu poḻital. mēkaṅkaḷ maḻai poḻivatu kālutal eṉappaṭum. tūrattil maḻai poḻiyumpōtu, tūṇkaḷ pōlap pala paṭalaṅkaḷākap pirintu vīḻvatai eṉṟum pārkkalām. av vīḻccikaḷ maḻaikkālkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ak kālkaḷ mēkattiṟku allatu vāṉattiṟkut tāṅkalpōlak kīḻē nīṇṭu nilattil viḻuvatupōl, uṟuppākiya kālum ōr uṭampiṟkut tāṅkalāyk kīḻē nīṇṭu nilattiṟ pativatāl, uvamaiyāku peyarāyk kāleṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉart tāṅkuvatu, aṭiyil iruppatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟik kāl eṉpatu pala poruḷ kaṭkup peyarāyiṟṟu. muḻaṅkāl  uṭampiṟ kāṟpakutiyātaliṉ kāleṉpatu nāliloru paṅkaiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. itaṟku maṟutalai yākak kūṟuvar ilakkaṇikaḷ.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akarātiyil (Lexicon) kāl eṉṉum  coṟku 44  poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaiyāvaṉa:-
 1. 	kāṟpaṅku	17. 	makaṉ
