kāl =	1. kāṟṟu
	       2. vātanōy
      	       3. pañcapūtam
kāl =	1 poḻutu	4. kālaṉ
	            2. cevvi	5. iyamaṉ
	            3. taṭavai	6. karuniṟam
kāl =	1. 7ām vēṟṟumai urupu
	           2. viṉaiyecca vikuti
	            3. ōr upacarkkam.
in nāṟpattu nāṉku poruḷkaḷum, kālpōlak kīḻiruppatu, 
tāṅkuvatu, nīṇṭiruppatu, pirintiruppatu, kāṟpaṅkāyiruppatu, nilattil ūṉṟuvatu mutaliya karuttuppaṟṟiyaṉavāyē iruttal  kāṇka.
kāl uṭampil ōr iṭamāyiruttalāl, ataṉ peyar iṭattaiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. pantarkkālukkup perumpālum mūṅkilai naṭuvatālum, mūṅkil vaḷarvatu kāṭṭilātalālum,  mūṅkiṟkuḻāyai aṟuttu mukattaṟ karuviyākak koḷvarātalālum, kāl eṉṉum peyar mūṅkil, vaṉam, marakkāl eṉṉum poruḷkaḷaiyum tarum.
poḻutu mutaliya mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷum kālattaiyum, kālaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟu peyarkaḷum maḻaikkālaiyum mūlamākak koṇṭaṉa vākum.
āṅkilattil ‘column’ eṉṉum col tūṇaiyum pirivaiyum caṭṭattaiyum kuṟikkum. caṭṭamum kāl, tūṇ, kālvāy ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaip pōṉṟē irukkum. kāl eṉṉum col kālattaik kuṟikkumpōtu tamiḻilum am īṟu peṟum. tūṇ eṉpatu tūṇam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ākaiyāl kāl ‘column’ eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku ōr iyaipuṇṭō eṉṟu aiyuṟakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. āṅkila akarātikaḷ ‘column’ eṉṉum coṟku celsus(high), collis(a hill) mutaliya mūlaṅkaḷaik   kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avai avvaḷavu poruttamuṭaiyaṉavalla. moḻi nūṟkalai iṉṉum cariyāy vaḷarkkappaṭāmaiyiṉ, mēṉāṭṭār tamiḻiṉ arumaiyai aṟintilar eṉṟē kūṟalvēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, cilar, tamiḻukku nīṇṭa kālattaik kuṟikkac collillai yeṉṟum, putaṉ, caṉi, rutu eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkut teṉ collillai yeṉṟum, 27 naṭcattiraṅkaḷukkum, 12 mātaṅkaṭkum tamiḻiṟ peyarillai yeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟit tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkā teṉṟum tamiḻarkkut taṉi nākarikamillai yeṉṟum, palavāṟu pitaṟṟāniṟpar. iṉi, cilar muḻuppūcaṉiyaic cōṟṟil maṟaippatupōl, tamiḻē vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatutāṉ eṉṟu varampiṟantu maṉappāl  kuṭippar. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku mika muntiya teṉpatum, vaṭamoḻit tōṟṟattiṟku allatu āriyar varukaikku muṉṉarē, akkālattu makkaṭkut tōṉṟiya karuttukaḷai yellām terivikkum coṟkaḷait taṉpāṟ koṇṭuḷḷa teṉpatum avar aṟiyār pōlum!
āriyar in nāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē tamiḻar pala kalaikaḷilum tērntiruntatiṉālum, tamiḻarkku vāṉanūlum (Astronomy) terin tiruntamai paṇṭai nūlkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvatālum, noṭi mutal ūḻiyīṟāṉa ciṟiya periya kāla aḷavukaṭkellām tamiḻiṟ colluṇmai yālum, colvaḷam tamiḻiṉ taṉip paṇpātalālum, kāl eṉpatu oru viṉaiyecca vikutiyākavum, kālam eṉpatu viṉaiyiṉ kālattaik kuṟikkuñ collākavum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻilakkaṇattil vaḻaṅkivaruvatālum, kāl, kālai, kālam eṉṉum mūṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum kuṟuṅkālattaiyum neṭuṅkālattaiyum kuṟikkat tamiḻil iruvakai vaḻakkilum peruvaḻakkākat toṉṟutoṭṭē vaḻaṅkivaruvatālum, kālam eṉṉum colliṉ mūlapporuḷ  tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟuvatālum, mēṉāṭṭāriya moḻikaḷil kālam eṉṉum col kālapporuḷil vaḻaṅkāmaiyāṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyapaṭi kālam eṉṉuñ col centamiḻc collē yeṉṟu eṭṭuṇaiyum aiyamaṟat teḷiyappaṭum.
tamiḻiṟ cila karuttukaḷai yuṇarttaṟku itupōtu collillai  yeṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āyiṉum ataṟkut takunta kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.
1. kiṟittuvukku muṟpaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷ, tolkāppiyam tavira maṟṟavaiyellām aḻintu pōyiṉamaiyiṉ, avaṟṟiluḷḷa aruñcoṟkaḷum maṟaintupōyiṉa.
2. paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyaik kaṭal koṇṭamai yiṉ, ap pakutiyil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṇṇiṟanta ticaiccoṟkaḷ aṟavē maṟaintaṉa.
3. tamiḻar tam aṟiyāmaipaṟṟi arumaiyāṉa teṉcoṟkaṭkup  patilāka, vaṭacoṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉatiṉālum,paṇṭaik kālattil virivāṉa tamiḻakarātikaḷ eḻutappaṭāmaiyālum, vaḻakkaṟṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām maṟaintoḻintaṉa.
4. tamiḻ, maṉita camūkattiṉ iḷamaiyil tōṉṟiya mikap paḻaimai yāṉa moḻiyātaliṉ, ikkālattiṟkuriya cila karuttukaḷ akkālat tamiḻarkkut tōṉṟavillai. ataṉāl avaṟṟukkuc collun tōṉṟavillai.
5. ceṉṟa 2000 āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻ āriyavayappaṭṭuḷḷamai yiṉ, putuccoṟkaḷ puṉaintu taṉittamiḻ vaḷarkkappaṭavillai.
ākaiyāl, tamiḻaip piṟa puṉmoḻikaḷpōṟ karuti iḻittuk kūṟuvatu takātu.
oru poruḷai ārāyumpōtu, viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi naṭuvu  nilaiyāy ārāytal vēṇṭum.
kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi oruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭaiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum.	(aṟaneṟi: 42)
mēlum, vaṭacol teṉcoṟkaḷaip pirittuṇartaṟku moḻinūl, Fyüš(Ethnology), carittira nūl mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉ tuṇai iṉṟiyamai yātu vēṇṭappaṭum. ākaiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ  tuṇaikoṇṭu uṇmai yaṟintu kaṭaippiṭikka. iṉṟēṟ kuñcaran taṭaviya kuruṭar kūṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉka.
piṟaṉpaḻi kūṟuvāṉ taṉpaḻi yuḷḷum
tiṟaṉterintu kūṟap paṭum. 	(kuṟaḷ. 186)
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' naḷi 1939
8
`māṇavaṉ' teṉcollā? vaṭacollā?
tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyamaiyālum, āriyam nāvalan tēyattiṟ pukumuṉṉarē tamiḻ muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntamaiyālum, intiya nākarikattiṉ cāramāṉa pakuti tamiḻ nākarikamē yātalālum, tamiḻuk kiṉṟiyamaiyāta paḻañ coṟkaḷellām taṉittamiḻē eṉpatu muḻu vuṟutiyāṉa uṇmaiyākum. āyiṉum, tamiḻaṉiṉ tāḻvuṇarcciyālum, ārāycciyiṉmaiyālum, āriya ēmāṟṟattālum iṉṉum tamiḻiṉ perumai tamiḻarālum aṟiyappaṭavillai.
teṉcollā vaṭacollā eṉṟu aiyuṟappaṭum coṟkaṭku, kīḻkkāṇum nilaimaikaḷiṟ pala vottiruppiṉ teṉcolleṉṟum, irāviṭiṉ vaṭacolleṉṟum tuṇiyalām.
1.	vērum vērpporuḷum tamiḻiliruttal.
2.	kiḷaiccoṟkaḷum vaḻiccoṟkaḷum, tamiḻil maṭṭu māyiṉum tamiḻilum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilumāyiṉum, ciṟu peruṅ kulamākavō kuṭumpamākavō iruttal.
3.	caṅkanūlkaḷil āṭcipeṟṟiruttal, allatu toṉṟu toṭṭa ulakavaḻakkil vaḻaṅkivaral.
4.	tamiḻeḻuttukaḷāl (tamiḻolikaḷāl) amaintiruttal.
5.	nāṭṭuppuṟattuk kallāmakkaḷāl perumpāṉmaiyākavō ciṟappākavō vaḻaṅkappeṟal.
6.	(aiyuṟappaṭum collāl) kuṟikkappeṟum poruḷ allatu karuttut tamiḻnāṭṭil allatu tamiḻar uḷḷat til toṉṟutoṭṭē (aḵtāvatu āriyar varumuṉ piruntē) iruntuvaral.
7.	colvaṭivu allatu collamaiti tamiḻukkēṟṟal.
 iṉi, māṇavaṉ eṉṉum collukkuc ciṟuvaṉ eṉpatē vērp poruḷām. makkaḷ āciriyaṉiṭattuk kaṟkum paruvam ciṟuparuvamātalāl, ciṟuvaṉaik kuṟikkum collē kaṟpōṉaiyum kuṟittatu. iṉṟum, piḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟum, paḷḷip piḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟum māṇavar aḻaikkap peṟutal kāṇka.
āṅkilattilum, ciṟuvaṉaik kuṟikkum cila coṟkaḷ māṇavaṉai yuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
Pupil.n. (L. pupillus, pupilla, dim.s. of pupas, a boy; pupa, a girl.) 1. A young person of either sex under the care of an instructor or tutor; a scholar; a disciple.	
	-The Imperial Dictionary, p. 573.
Pedagogue. n. (Gk. paidagogus-pais, paidos, a child and ago, to lead') A teacher of children; one whose occupation is to instruct young children; a school master now used generally by way of contempt							      Ibid. p. 396.
māṇavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ aṭi (Stem) maṇ eṉpatākum. iv vaṭiyiṉ vēr (Root) muḷ eṉpatu. iv vēriṉ iyalpuvaṭivam mul.
mul eṉpatu, ciṟumaiyai allatu iḷamaiyai uṇarttum vērc coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
mul-(muṉ)-muṉi	=	(kaṉṟu), yāṉaikkaṉṟu
		``muṉiyuṭaik kavaḷam pōla'' (naṟ.360).
mul - muḷ - muḷai =	1. iḷamai
		``muḷaiyamai tiṅkaḷ'' (kamparā. 					kumpakarṇaṉ varaip paṭalam, 16.)
	2. marakkaṉṟu. ``ataṉṟāḷ vaḻiyē 				muḷaiyōṅkupu'' (cīvaka. 223).
	3. ciṟukuḻantai.
muḷai - muḷaiyāṉ = ciṟukuḻantai.
muḷ - muḷi - muṟi = taḷir. ``muṟimēṉi'' (kuṟaḷ. 1113)
muṟital = tuḷirttal. ``muṟinta kōla'' (cīvaka. 2358)
muṟi - maṟi = cila vilaṅkiṉ kuṭṭi
``yāṭum kutiraiyum navviyum uḻaiyum
ōṭum pulvāy uḷappaṭa maṟiyē.''	(tol. 1511)
muḷ - muṭṭu = ciṟu poruḷ. muṭṭukkāy = ciṟukāy.
muṭṭuk kurumpai = ciṟu kurumpai. muṭṭu - muṭṭai = ciṟu karaṇṭi.
muṭṭai = moṭṭai. moṭṭaip payal = ciṟu payal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭukku = ciṟu cantu.
moḻivaḷarcciyil coṟkaḷ aṭaiyum palvakait tiripukaḷuḷ, iṉattiripu eṉpatu oṉṟu. atil uyiriṉat tiripu eṉpatu oru pirivu.  ōruyir maṟṟō ruyirākattirivatu uyiriṉat tiripām. enta vuyirum vēṟenta uyirākavum tiriyalām. āyiṉum, perumpāṉmaiyāka iyalkiṉṟa cila muṟaippaṭṭa uyiriṉat tiripukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ       u-a eṉpatu oṉṟu. 
e-ṭu:	kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu	tuṇai - taṉai
	kuṭam - kaṭam	muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku
	kuṭṭai - kaṭṭai	muṟi - maṟi (=vaḷai)
	tuḷir - taḷir
muḷ - maḷ
maḷ-maḷḷaṉ = iḷaiñaṉ.
``poruviṟal maḷḷa'' (tirumuruku. 262)
maḷ - maḻa
``rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā'' (tol, 49)
ātalāl, maḻ eṉṉum vaṭivam tōṉṟātu,
maḻa =	1. iḷamai.
	``maḻavum kuḻavum iḷamaip poruḷa'' (tol, 796)
	2. kuḻantai.
	``aḻumaḻap pōlum'' (tirukkō. 147)
maḻa - maḻavu = iḷamai, kuḻantai.
maḻapulavar = paḷḷiyiṟ paṭikkum ciṟār.
``maiyāṭa lāṭaṉ maḻapulavar māṟeḻuntu'' (paripā. 11 : 88)
maḻamaḻappu = meṉmai.
maḻalai= iḷamai, meṉmoḻi, kuḻantaimoḻi, putumai
maḻalaittēṉ = pututtēṉ.
maḻavaṉ =	1. iḷaiñaṉ (piṅ.).
		``maḻavarta maṉaiyaṉa maṇavoli'' (kamparā. nāṭṭup 				 paṭalam, 50)
		2. vīraṉ ``maḻavar peruma'' (puṟam. 90)
maḻaṟutal = meṉmaiyātal.
``maḻaṟutēṉ moḻiyārkaḷ'' (tiv. tiruvāymoḻi, 6 : 2 : 5.)
maḻa-māḻai= iḷamai.
``māḻai maṭamāṉ piṇaiyiyal'' (kalit. 131)
maḻa-(maḻai)-vaḻai=putumai.
``pākariṟai vaḻaimatu nukarpu'' (paripā, 11 : 66.)
ma - va, meyyiṉat tiripu. o.nō: miñcu-viñcu.
maḻa - maḻu - maḻukkai - vaḻukkai. vaḻukkaippayal = ciṟu payal.
vaḻukkai - vaḻukkaṭṭai = ciṟu piḷḷai.
vaḻai - vaḻaiccu = putumai.
``cāṭiyiṉ vaḻaiccaṟa viḷainta'' (perumpā. 280)
putumai ciṟumai aḻaku vīram mutaliyaṉa iḷamaiyoṭu toṭar puṭaiya karuttukaḷ. ataṉāl, iḷamaiyaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ ip poruḷkaḷai uṇarttiṉa.
maḻalai - matalai = kuḻantai moḻi, kuḻantai, makaṉ.
matalaikkiḷi = iḷaṅkiḷi.
maḻa - (mata) - maṭa - maṭam = iḷamai, meṉmai, aḻaku, aṟiyāmai.
maṭamai =	1. meṉmai. ``teḷinaṭai maṭappiṇai'' (puṟam. 23)
		2. aṟiyāmai
aṟivu perumpālum āṇṭup perukkattāl aṭaiyap peṟuvatāl, iḷamaiyil aṟiyāmai mikuntiruppatu iyalpu. itaṉāl, iḷamaiccol aṟiyāmaiyai uṇarttiṟṟu.
maḷ - (maṇ) - maṇi = ciṟiyatu.
maṇikkayiṟu, maṇikkāṭai, maṇikkuṭal, maṇikkai, maṇikkōrai, maṇiccampā, maṇiccuṟā, maṇittakkāḷi, maṇittutti,  maṇippayal, maṇippuṉku, maṇippuṟā, maṇippoccam, maṇiyīral mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
maṇ - māṇ -	1, ciṟuvaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ, maṇavātāṉ (piramacāri).
	``māṇāki vaiya maḷantatuvum'' (tiv. periyatiru moḻi; 8 : 10 : 8,)
	2. kuṟaḷ, kuṟaḷaṉ.
	``kuṟumā ṇuruvaṉ taṟkuṟiyāk koṇṭāṭum''
	(tēvāram 164 : 5)
	māṇ - māṇi = 1. maṇavātāṉ, māṇavaṉ
	``karumāṇi yāyiranta kaḷvaṉē'' (tiv.iyaṟpā. 2:61)
	2. kuṟaḷ vaṭivam
	(tiv, periyāḻvār tirumoḻi, 1 : 4 : 1 svāpatēca viyākkiyāṉam).
	3. ciṟṟuṟuppu.
māṇi eṉṉum col kaṉṉaṭattilu muḷatu. māṇ, māṇi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi lillai.
māṇ - māṇavaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟpōṉ.
māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ = iḷaiñaṉ, kaṟpōṉ, maṇavātāṉ, ciṟuvaṉ, aṟivīṉaṉ.
yāḻppāṇa akarātiyil, māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum collukku 8 mutal 16 āṇṭukkuṭpaṭṭa ciṟuvaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷatu.
potuvākak kalvikaṟkum paruvam maṇattiṟku muntiyatātalālum, maṇapparuvam āṭavaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇṭeṉṟu akapporu ḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatālum, 8 āṇṭu mutal 16 āṇṭuvaraippaṭṭa iḷaiñaṉai māṇavakaṉ eṉṟu aḻaippatu tamiḻmarapiṟkē mikap poruttamām. iṉṟum, vacatiyuḷḷa āṭavarkkellām patiṉāṟām āṇṭilēyē maṇam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. āriyamaṇam eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟāṉa piramam eṉpatu, ``nāṟpatteṭṭiyāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttavaṉukkup paṉṉīrāṭṭaip paruvattāḷāyp pūppu eytiyavaḷaip peyarttu iraṇṭām pūppu eytāmuṉ aṇikalaṉaṇintu tāṉamākak koṭuppatu'' (tol. poruḷ. 92, urai) tamiḻaṉ 48 āṇṭuvarai maṇavātiruttal mika aritākum. veppanāṭṭār muntiyum taṭpanāṭṭār pintiyum maṇattal iyaṟkai viḷaivākum. iṉṟum, mēṉāṭṭār perumpālum nāṟpatiyāṇṭu nirampiya piṉṉarē maṇakkiṉṟaṉar. intiya āriyariṉ muṉṉōr ātiyil vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkumuḷḷa kuḷirnāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, avarkku nāṟpatiyāṇṭu maṇavātirukka muṭintatu. tamiḻarkkō in nilai aṉṟumillai; iṉṟumillai ātalāl, iḷaṅkaṟṟuccolliyai māṇavakaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻvaḻakkēyākum.
māṇavakam = kalvi (cintāmaṇi nikaṇṭu)
māṇavaṉ - (māṇakaṉ) - (māṇakkaṉ) - māṇākkaṉ.
māṇavi māṇākki eṉpaṉa peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
māṇavaṉ māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum īr āṇ peyarum vaṭamoḻiyil, muṟaiyē, māṇava māṇavaka eṉa īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. māṇavikā eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peṇpāṟ peyar. māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl vaṭivamum māṇavi  māṇākki eṉṉum peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷum māṇavaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭivaḻiyāṉa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyi lillai. ṉakaravīṟṟut tamiḻp peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ perumpālum īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu iyalpu.
tolkāppiyattiṟku āttirēyaṉ pērāciriyaṉ vakutta potup pāyirattil,
``iṉṉō raṉṉa toṉṉeṟi marapiṉar
palperuñ ciṟappiṉ nallā ciriyar
....................................................
uṭaiyō rāki naṭaiyaṟin toḻukunar
naṉmā ṇākkareṉpa.....................''

eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āciriyaṉ, māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭum taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
māṇavaṉ, māṇavakaṉ, māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum mūvaṭivaṅkaḷum, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷālēyē amaintavai.
āriyar varumuṉpē tamiḻar kalviyil tuṟaipōyiruntaṉar. āciriya māṇava marapil kalvi vaḻaṅkivantatu.
``uṭaiyārmuṉ illārpōl ēkkaṟṟuṅ kaṟṟār
kaṭaiyarē kallā tavar.'' 	(kuṟaḷ. 395)
``cevikkuṇa villāta pōḻtu ciṟitu
tyiṟṟukkuÛyapgṭum.''		(Fṟaḷ.412)
eṉpavai piṟkālattavaiyāyiṉum, muṟkāla nilaimaiyaiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka. iṉi,
``otta cūttiram uraippiṉ kāṇṭikai
meyppaṭak kiḷanta vakaiya tāki'' 	(tol. 1598)
``cūttirat tuṭporuḷ aṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
iṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellām
oṉṟa uraippatu uraiyeṉap paṭumē.'' 	(tol. 1603)
mutaliya pakutikaḷum, muṟkāṭṭiya potuppāyirap pakutiyum, āciriya vāyiṟ kalviyai uṇarttutal kāṇka. āciriya māṇavar āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭaikkālattē jmiḻnāṭṭiliruntatiṉālmvaraikFṟittaṟkucbcāṟkaḷumgḻantamiḻilïruntiruttalnvaṇṭum.f‰ngh‹, koḷvōṉ, kōḷāḷaṉ, kēṭpōṉ mutaliya māṇavaṉ bpayarkaḷum;fṟpippōṉ,Evalvōṉ,craippōṉKtaliyaMciriyaṉbpayarfḷum;Nttiraṅkaḷilumbcayyuḷkaḷilumnmāṉaiyetukaiknkaṟṟavāṟummaikkappeṟumbpātucbcāṟkaḷēyaṉṟi,krapubnaṟippaṭṭaáṟappucbcāṟkaḷalla.f‰W¢brhšÈ eṉpatu iṭaikkālatteḻunta toṭarmoḻip         peyar. māṇavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ amaippum tamiḻmuṟaippaṭṭatē. māṇ, pakuti; avaṉ, vikuti. ūṉavaṉ, kāṉavaṉ, vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka. ivaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷai orukāl ūṉam, kāṉam mutaliya makara vīṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākak koḷḷiṉum, āṉavaṉ kōṉavaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷai aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷa muṭiyāmai kāṇka.
āṉavar = iṭaiyar (aka. ni.).
māṇavakaṉ eṉpatu māṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ virittal. māṇākkaṉ eṉpatu māṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ tirittal. māṇavaṉ - māṇavi, māṇākkaṉ - māṇākki eṉa āṇpāṟpeyar aṉṉīṟum, peṇpāṟpeyar ikaravīṟum peṟṟiruppatu, tamiḻukkuc ciṟappām.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, teṉcoṟku vitikkappaṭṭa eḻu nilaimaikaḷuḷ āṟu (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7) māṇavaṉ eṉṉum collukku ēṟṟal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacoṟkaḷē eṉṉum kuruṭṭuk koḷkaiyai nampi, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa tuṇaiyāṉē teṉcoṟkaḷai yellām vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉa mayaṅkaṟka. vaṭamoḻicceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu māṇavaṉ eṉpatu. tamiḻil cīṭaṉ eṉṟu taṟpavamāki vaḻaṅkum ciṣya eṉṉum collē vaṭacollākum.
itaṉaiyum itupōṉṟa piṟa vuṇmaikaḷaiyum uṇartaṟku, tamiḻaiyum tamiḻaraiyum tamiḻnāṭṭaiyum paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuṇmaikaḷai aṟital vēṇṭum. yām aṇmaiyil veḷiyiṭum mutaṟṟāymoḻi, vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu, mutal nākarikam mutaliya nūlkaḷuḷ avaṟṟaip parakkak kaṇṭu teḷika.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' navampar 1949
9
eṉ peyar eṉcol?
moḻiyaip poṟuttavaraiyil nāṉ maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷai muṟṟum aṭiyoṟṟic celpavaṉāyiruntum, tamiḻp pērāciriyar palarum moḻiyārāycciyiṉmaiyāṉē eṉ peyaraiyum vaṭacolleṉa aiyuṟṟum varukiṉṟaṉar. avar mayakkaṟuppāṉ eḻuntatik kaṭṭurai.
1. tēvaṉ
ekkālattum māntar teyva vaḻipāṭāṟṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam, naṉmai ceyyum poruḷiṭattu aṉpum, tīmaiceyyum poruḷiṭattu accamumē. ellām valla iṟaivaṉ vaḻipāṭṭil iv viru meyppāṭukaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa. iṟaivaṉ iṟutiyil pēriṉpan taruvāṉ eṉpatu naṉmai yum, erinirayattil iṭuvāṉ eṉpatu tīmaiyum paṟṟiya uṇarccikaḷākum.
veḷiyoḻinta nāṟpūtaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum naṉmaiyum tīmaiyuñ ceyyumēṉum, avaṟṟuḷ tīyē avaṟṟaic ciṟappāka allatu teḷivākac ceyvatām. ataṉāl, mutaṟkāla māntar ataṉaiyē ciṟanta teyvamākap pōṟṟivantaṉar. atupaṟṟit teyvap potuppeyarum, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭpeyarum tīppeyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭpulaṉaip payaṉpaṭuvikkum oḷiyum, camaiyaṟku vēṇṭum cūṭum, kuḷiraip pōkkum vemmaiyum, uṇavup payirkku vēṇṭum veppamum, tīyiṉāl uṇṭākum naṉmaikaḷām; tīṇṭiṉāṟ cuṭuvatum, akappaṭiṉ erittuk kolvatum, ataṉāl viḷaivum tīmaikaḷām. nāṟ pūtaṅkaḷuḷḷum, ciṟṟaḷaviliruppiṉum tīmai ceyvatu tīyēyātaliṉ, tīmaippeyar ataṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṟṟu.
marattoṭu maramum kalloṭu kallum uracumpōtum neriyum pōtum neruppuṇṭāvataik kaṇṭa mutaṟkāla māntar, avvakai yilēyē neruppuṇṭākkak kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl, uracal, nerical, tēytal mutaliya urāytaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, tīyaik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uracu, urāy, urāyñcu, uriñ, uriñu, uriñcu, urai, uraicu, uraiñcu, urōcu, urōñcu eṉpaṉa oruvērp piṟanta oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
ur: ur - uru. uruttal = aḻalutal.
``ākam uruppa nūṟi'' (puṟam. 25 : 10)
uru - urum - urumpu = kotippu.
``urumpil kūṟṟat taṉṉa'' (patiṟṟup. 26 : 13).
uru - urumam = veppam. naṇpakal.
urumakālam = kōṭaikālam.
urumittal = puḻuṅkutal,
uru - uruppu = veppam.
``kaṉmicai uruppiṟak kaṉaituḷi citaṟeṉa'' (kalit. 16 : 7)
uruppu - uruppam = veppam.
``kaṉalum................... uruppameḻa'' (aric. pu. vivā. 104)
uru - uruku - urukku - urukkam.
urukutal = veppattiṉāl iḷakutal, maṉam iḷakutal (iraṅkutal).
urukku = urukiṉa irumpu (eḵku).
urukkāṅkal = urukippōṉa ceṅkal.
ur - uri = neruppu (kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷuvam).
uri eṉṉum vaṭivam tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟu maṟaintatu.
uri - eri = neruppu.
neruppaik kuṟikkum `uru' vēr tamiḻccoṟkaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
ilattīṉ (Latin) - ur, (to burn)
	armīṉiyam (Armenian) ōr = neruppu.
	āpukkāṉiyam (Afghan) - ōr, vur = neruppu.
	epirēyam (Hebrew) ūr = neruppu, ōr = oḷi.
ner:	ner - neri - nerital = neruṅkutal, uracutal, noṟuṅkutal.
	ner - neru - neruppu. neruppu - nippu (teluṅku).
	arapiyam (Arabic) - nār = neruppu, nūr = oḷi.
tēy:	tēytal = uracutal.
	tēy - tīy - tī = neruppu, viḷakku, narakam.
	tī - tīmai = tīyiṉtaṉmai. tī. tīya = koṭiya,
	tīytal = erintupōtal, karukutal, paṟṟippōtal,
	tītal = tīytal.
ēkāram īkāramāyt tiritalai, tēṉ - tēm - tīm eṉṉum tiripilum kaṇṭukoḷka.
tēy - tēyu (camaṟkirutam) = neruppu.
it `tēy' aṭiyiṉiṉṟē teyvappeyar tōṉṟiṟṟu.
tēy - tēyvu - tēvu - tēvaṉ,
tēyvu - teyvu - teyvam.
tēvu - tē = teyvam, talaivaṉ,
``pālvarai teyvam viṉaiyē pūtam'' (tol. 541)
``teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟai'' (tol. 964).
``teyvam añcal puraiyaṟan teḷital'' (tol. 1218)
``vaḻipaṭu teyvam niṟpuṟaṅ kāppa'' (tol. 1367).
yakaravoṟṟuḷḷa teyvap peyarē perumpāṉmaiyākavum, 
``tēvarp parāaya muṉṉilaik kaṇṇē'' (tol. 1395)
eṉa vakaravoṟṟuḷḷa piṉṉai vaṭivu arukiyum, itupōtuḷḷa tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷ muntiyatākiya tolkāppiyattuḷ varutal kāṇka.
camaṟkirutam - deva, daiva
ilattīṉ - dues = god.
kirēkkam (Greek) - theos = god.
`tēva' eṉṉum coṟkuc camaṟkirutattiṟ kāṭṭappaṭum div (to be bright). di, dip (to shine) eṉṉum vērccoṟkaḷ mutaṟporuḷaṉṟi vaḻipporuḷē koṇṭuḷḷamaiyiṉ poruntāmai kāṇka.
piṉvarum cittiya (Scythian) āriyac coṟkaḷ, `tī' (neruppu) eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟku iṉamāṉavai eṉak kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kāṭṭuvār.
cittiyam:
cāmāyitē (samoiede) - tu, tui, ti, ty.
mañcu (Manchu) - tua.
aṅkēriyam (Hungarian) - tuz.
ōcuttiyakku (Ostiak) - tut.
tuṅkacu (Tungus) - togo.
ilacukkiyam (Lesghian) - tze, zi, zie.
āriyam :
	kēlikkam (Gaelic) - teire
vēlicu (Welsh) - tan
pāracīkam (Persian) - tigh
2. nēyaṉ
naḷ :	naḷ - naṇ - naṇpu - naṭpu.
naḷḷutal = aṭaital, poruntutal, naṭpuk koḷḷutal, ceṟital.
naḷḷār = pakaivar.
naḷḷi = uṟavu (cūṭā.).
naḷḷiruḷ = ceṟintaviruḷ (tirukkō. 156, cilap. 15 : 105)
naḷḷunar = naṇpar (tivā.).
naḷ - naḷi. naḷital = ceṟital.
naḷ - neḷ - ney = oṭṭum nīrpporuḷ (oil, ghee),
	karuppukkaṭṭic cāntu.
neytal = iṇaittal, nūlai iṇaittu āṭaiyākac ceytal.
ney = kuruti, neyttōr (neyttuvar?) = kuruti,
akaram ekaramātalai, paru - peru, cattāṉ - cettāṉ eṉṉum tiripukaḷiṟ kāṇka.
ḷakara meyyīṟu yakara meyyīṟāvatai, koḷ - koy, toḷ - toy, poḷ - poy mutaliya tiripukaḷiṟ kāṇka.
ney - (neyñcu) - neñcu - neñcam = vilāvelumpukaḷ iṇaikkappaṭṭa iṭam allatu aṉpiṟkiṭamākak karutappaṭum neñcāṅkulai (Heart) uḷḷa iṭam.
ney - nēy - nēyam = aṉpu.
īram, pacai mutaliya coṟkaḷ oṭṭum poruḷaiyum aṉpaiyum kuṟittal kāṇka. aṉpu, iruvarai allatu palarai iṇaippatu.
nēy - nē = aṉpu. nēyam - nēcam.
ya - ca. o.nō: neyavu - necavu.
nēcam - nēci. nēcittal = aṉpu kaṭāttal virumputal.
``nēcittu racavāta vittaik kalaintiṭuvar'' (tāyumāṉavar)
nēyam - nēyaṉ. nēcam - nēcaṉ.
nēyam eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭattil vaḻaṅki, piṉṉarp pirākirutattilum toṭarntu, iṟutiyil camaṟkirutattiṟ pukuntu `nēha' eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iv varalāṟṟumuṟai aṟiyātār, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatupōl, nēha (camaṟ) - nēyam (pirākirutam) - nēyam (tamiḻ) eṉat talaikīḻāyt tirippar. iṅṅaṉamē ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyilum, uṇmaikku māṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pakuttaṟivum naṭuvunilaimaiyumuḷḷa aṟiñar uṇmai kaṇṭukoḷvārāka. nēyam vaṭacollāyiṉ `ney' eṉpatum ataṉ aṭivērāṉa `naḷ' eṉpatum vaṭacollātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmai veḷḷiṭaimalai pōl teḷḷitē.
vaṭamoḻi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭiruppatāl, moḻinūṉ muṟaippaṭi naṭuvunilaiyāy ārāyntu uṇmai kāṇavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyātalāl piṟamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉkoḷḷāteṉṉum karunāṭakak kuruṭṭuk koḷkaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, tūya teṉcoṟkaḷaiyum vaṭacolleṉa valippatu, aṟivārāycci mikka ikkālattiṟ kēṟkāteṉa, vaṭamoḻi vāṇar tiṭamāka aṟivārāka; ārāycciyillāta tamiḻp pērāciriyarum, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa collellām vaṭacolleṉak karutum pētai maiyai viṭṭuyvārāka. mayir eṉṉum teṉcol `cmacru' (smrsru) eṉac cakaram mutaṟkoṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟē, nēyam eṉṉum teṉ collum `nēha' eṉa sakaram muṟkoṇṭu camaṟkirutattil vaḻaṅku kiṉṟateṉa aṟika.
- ``teṉmoḻi'' navampar 1959
10
cilai eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu
uruvam, cilai, paṭimai eṉṉum oruporuḷ muccoṟkaḷuḷ mutalatum īṟṟatum aiyuṟavaṟṟa tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ.
uruvam
uru eṉṉum vaṭivum uruvattaik kuṟikkum.
urukkal = kōvil kaṭṭiyavaruṭaiya kaṟcilai. itu kaṭṭaṭak kalai vaḻakku.
uruvam = cilai (piratimai).
uruvāram = cilai (piratimai) itu ulaka vaḻakku.
uruvam eṉpatu rūpa eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉpar. itu ēmāṟṟu.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal.
uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivuṭaip poruḷ, paṭimai.
uru - uruvu - urupu =vēṟṟumai vaṭivam; ataik kuṟikkum eḻuttu allatu acai allatu col.
uruvam eṉṟa teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil rūpa eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
paṭimai
paṭi = oppu, vakai, uruvam (cīvaka. 1156). appaṭi =atupōl, avvakai. orupaṭiyāy (vakaiyāy) varukiṟatu. oru vaṭiyāy eṉpatu nellai vaḻakku.
paṭiyōlai = mūla motta ōlai.
paṭi = prati (va.) itaiyum prati > paṭi eṉat talaikīḻāyk kāṭṭi ēmāṟṟuvar vaṭavar.
paṭi - paṭimai - pratimā (va.) - patumai (taṟpavam).
paṭimai - paṭimam = nōṉpuk kōlam, tavavaṭivam.
(paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam; paṭivu - vaṭivu; paṭivam - vaṭivam.)
cilai
cilai eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil kal allatu pāṟai eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. Silex eṉṉum ilattīṉ collum, ataṉiṉṟu tirinta silica eṉṉum āṅkilac collum vaṉkallai (second stone) yaṉṟi uruvac cilaiyaik kuṟiyā. nāmtāṉ ataik karuviyākupeyarāy uruvam eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
cilā cācaṉam = kallil eḻutappaṭṭa kaṭṭaḷai, kaṟpaṭṭayam, vikkirakam, mūrttam eṉpaṉavē uruvattaik kuṟikkum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. mūrttam uṭaiyavaṉ mūrtti. pratimā eṉpatu teṉcoṟṟiripu.
nām kaṟcilai eṉpatupōl vaṭavar cilācilā eṉṉa muṭiyātu iruppuccilai, veḷḷiccilai, ceppuccilai, poṟcilai, veṇkalac cilai eṉṟu uruvapporuḷiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. kaṟporuḷil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatillai. teyva uruvam teyvaccilai eṉappaṭum.
vaṭamoḻiyil kal eṉṉum poruṭkum vēr allatu mutaṉilai illai. māṉiyar villiyamcu tam akaramutaliyil, orukāl `ci' mūlamāyirukkalām eṉpar. `ci' eṉpataṟku nalku, pontikai (tirupti)p paṭuttu, kūrākku eṉṉum poruḷkaḷtām uṇṭu. avai poruntā. tamiḻilum tiṭṭamākavō, tēṟṟamākavō mūlaṅkāṭṭutaṟ killai. cilai eṉṉuñ coṟkuc cil eṉpatutāṉ mūlamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cil = tuṇṭu, vaṭṭam, vaṭṭapporuḷ.
cilleṉal = kuḷirtal, olittal.
cil - cilai = vaḷainta vil, olikkum etiroli.
cil - cilampu = olikkum kaḻal (paral iṭappaṭṭa kālaṇi), etiroliyiṭum malai, olikkum cilampak kaḻi. (cilampu - cilampam).
cilaittal = olittal, muḻaṅkutal, āravārittal - `malaipaṭukaṭām' ikkaruttatu. pāṭumpōtu etirolittalaic cilaiyōṭutal eṉpar nellai nāṭṭār.
cil eṉpatu vaṭṭam eṉṉum karuttil cul eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
culavutal = vaḷaital. tuṇṭu eṉṉum karuttutāṉ cil eṉpataic cilaiyōṭu iṇaikka ōraḷavu utavum.
cil - cilukku = veṭṭiṉa tuṇṭu; cilukku - citukku - cetukku.      (la - ta pōli) o.nō: calaṅkai - cataṅkai; kalampam - katampam; mel - metu.
cil - cilai = veṭṭiṉa allatu cetukkiṉa uruvam. ātalāl, kal eṉṉum poruḷ tamiḻil illai.
iṉi, cilai eṉpatu cilampu eṉpatu pōl malai eṉṟum tamiḻil poruḷ koṇṭiruppatāl, malai eṉṉum poruḷiṉiṉṟē kal (malaippiñcu) eṉṉum mutalākupeyarp poruḷum teṉmoḻiyilum, vaṭamoḻiyilum tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
cilai = malai - ``vaṇaṅkāc cilaiaḷitta tōḷāṉ'' (pu.ve. 2:12)
cilai = kal (piṅka.)
ākavē, cilai eṉṉum uruvapporuṭ coṟkut tamiḻ mūlam caṟṟu valintu koḷvatākavē iruppiṉum, piṉvarum ētukkaḷāl atait teṉcol eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
1.	vaṭamoḻiyil cilai eṉṉum coṟku mūlamillai.
2.	am moḻiyil ac coṟkuk kaṟporuḷēyaṉṟi uruvap poruḷillai. tamiḻil iraṇṭumuṇṭu.
3.	āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉpē tamiḻakattil uruvak kalai iruntatu.
4.	vaṭamoḻiyil kuṟaintapakkam aintiliru pakuti teṉcol. (eṉ 	`vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu' pārkka.)
5.	uruvam, paṭimai eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum vaṭacollākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai pōṉṟē cilai eṉpatum.
6.	vēta āriyarkku vēḷvi vaḻipāṭēyaṉṟi uruva vaṇakkamillai. teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉṉarē tamiḻariṭamiruntu mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.
7.	cilā eṉṉum vaṭacollil irumoḻip potuveḻuttē yaṉṟi vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttillai.
8.	tamiḻ aikāravīṟu vaṭamoḻiyil ākāra īṟāyt tirivatu iyalpē. e-ṭu: mālai - mālā; vaṭai - vaṭā.
9.	cilā eṉṉuñ col irukku vētattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa vaṭanūlkaḷilēyē āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10.	cilampu, cilai eṉṉum iru (malaipporuṭ) coṟkaḷum, tamiḻil cil eṉṉum orē mūlattaiyum, olittal eṉṉum orē vērpporuṭ karaṇiyattaiyum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
11.	tamiḻ mēlai āriyattiṟkum aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷai utaviya peruvaḷamoḻi.
- nākait tamiḻccaṅka maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ
niṉaivu malar 1969
11
karumam tamiḻc collē!
karumam eṉpatu, kumarik kaṇṭattil karu eṉṉum mutaṉilai yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, aṭināḷil airōppā ceṉṟa tamiḻar al, tiraviṭar moḻiyiṟ kalantiruntatumāṉa tūya tamiḻc collākum.
karumam = ceyal, toḻil, kaṭamai. R.V. karman
karu - karumai - karumam.
o.nō :	paru - parumai - parumam.
	uva - uvamai - uvamam.
makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ tamiḻilum ṉakara vīṟākat tiritaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu : parumam - parumaṉ. uvamam - uvamaṉ.
karu-ttal = ceytal. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa tamiḻ viṉaikaḷuḷ oṉṟām. aṭṭu, poṭṭu, kaḷ, naṉ mutaliya pala viṉaikaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamai kāṇka.
aṭṭutal = vārttal
poṭṭutal = poruttutal, kaṭṭutal
poruttu - pottu - poṭṭu
poṭṭu + aṇam - poṭṭaṇam.
o.nō :	kaṭṭu + aṇam = kaṭṭaṇam
	kaṭṭal = kaḷavu ceytal. kaḷḷāmai = kaḷavu ceyyāmai.
	naṭṭal = naṭpuc ceytal.
oruporuḷ paṟṟip pala colliyiruppiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum cilavum vaḻaṅkappaṭāmaṟ pōtal iyalpē.
karu-ttal eṉṉum coṟku iruvakaiyil vērp poruḷ kūṟalām.
1.	fUik = bgUik (âth.), mikuti. kaṭu - karu.
	kaṭuttal = mikutal. ``neñcaṅ kaṭuttaru'' (kuṟaḷ. 706)
	karuttal = muṉa illāta toṉṟu mikumāṟu ceytal, ceytal.
āṅkilattil make eṉṉum colliṟku, itē karuttil, magnus (L), megas (Gk.) eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mag eṉṉum vēraiyē mūlamākak kāṭṭutal kāṇka.
karumam - kammam. (> Pkt. kamma) = 1. kammiyar toḻil, smith's work `kammañ cey mākkaḷ' (nālaṭiyār, 393) 2.	uḻavut toḻil.
kammavār (kammam + avar) - uḻavut toḻil ceyyum oru teluṅka vakuppār.
kammam - kamam = uḻavut toḻil.
nākarika vulakil mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭṭa kaittoḻil uḻavut toḻilē. ataṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar uḻavaraik ``kaiceytūṇ mālai yavar'' (1035) eṉṟār.
kamam - kam =	1. uḻavut toḻil. 
	2. kammiyar toḻil, ``ī muṅ kammum'' (tol. eḻut. 328)
kammālai =	1. erutu pūṭṭiyirukkum iṟaivai yēṟṟam.
	2. kaṉṉār toḻiṟkūṭam.
kammālai - kamalai - kavalai.
kamalai eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kavalai eṉpatu cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kaṉṉaṭa teluṅku moḻikaḷil (ciṟappākak kaṉṉaṭattil, vakaram pakaramākat tirivatāl, kavalai eṉpatu avviru moḻikaḷilum kapile (kapile) eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaik kurāṟ peṟṟattaik kuṟikkum kapilā eṉṉum vaṭacollōṭu mayakki, (kavalaiyēṟṟattaik kapilaiyēṟṟamākak koṇṭu) kapilai eṉak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyāḷar. mutalāvatu, kapilaiyeṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil ōriṭattilum vaḻaṅkavillai. iraṇṭāvatu, kapilai eṉpatu kapila niṟamuḷḷa peṟṟam (pacu.) kamalaiyil kāḷaikaḷaik kaṭṭi nīriṟaipparē yaṉṟip peṟṟaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭi yiṟaiyār.
kamalai vaṇṭi, kamalaiyuruḷai, kamalaivaṭam, kamalaikkuḻi mutaliya palacoṟkaḷ kamalai yiṟaivaiyoṭu toṭarpuḷḷavai. ivaṟṟai mēṟkuṟitta akarātiyāciriyar aṟavum aṟintilar.
paṇṇutal eṉṉum collum itē karuttaṭippaṭaiyil amaintatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. paṇ - paṇṇai = kūṭṭam, peruṅkūṭṭam, tokuti, mikuti.
2. karuttal = kai karukkumāṟu oru viṉai ceytal, ceytal.
toṭarntu vēlai ceyvataṉāl, karu niṟattār kai karuppatum poṉṉiṟattār allatu veṇṇiṟattār kai civappatum iyalpu. `ceyta kai cēvēṟum' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. cēvu - cēku = cevvayiram.
cey - cemmai. ceytal - kai civakkumāṟu oru viṉai ceytal, ceytal.
ivviruvakai mūlaṅkaḷuḷ etu uṇmaiyāyiṉum, karumam eṉpatu tamiḻc collātal tēṟṟam. atu vaṭamoḻiyil tirinta mutaṉilaiyē yaṉṟi vērp poruḷ ētumillai.
karu + vi = karuvi. oru toḻil ceyvataṟku vēṇṭum mūlapporuḷ allatu tuṇaipporuḷ.
ic col tamiḻeṉpatai evarum maṟukkār. itu tamiḻāyiṉ itaṉoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa karumam eṉpatum tamiḻēyātal vēṇṭum.
karu + aṇam = karaṇam = ceyal. maṇac ceyaṟcaṭaṅku, ceyaṟku vēṇṭum karuvi.
karaṇam - karaṇiyam = aṟivup pulak karuviyāṉa akakkaraṇam.         R.V. karana.
karaṇam eṉpatai nīṭṭik kāraṇa eṉṟum, (ataṉoṭu poruntak) karu eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyai nīṭṭik kārya eṉṟum, vittum viḷaiyum pōlak karumamum paṟṟiya iru nuṇporuṭ coṟkaḷai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ivaṟṟait tamiḻiṟ cērttatiṉālēyē, karumam, karaṇam eṉṟum coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkutal paṟṟi vaṭacolleṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. kāraṇam, kāriyam eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum īṭāka, karaṇiyam, karumiyam eṉa iru coṟkaḷai amaittuk koḷḷalām.
kammak kuṭam = kammiyar (kaṉṉār) ceyta kuṭam.
kam - kammi = kuyavaṉ. ``maṭkalañ cey kammi'' (pārata. tirau. 64) kalañ ceyyum oppumaiyāl, kuyavaṉum kaṉṉāṉpōṟ karutappeṟṟāṉ.
kam - kammiyam = 1. kaittoḻil; 2. kammāḷar toḻil.
kammiyam- kammiyaṉ =	1. toḻilāḷi
	``kammiyaru mūrvar kaḷiṟu'' (cīvaka. 495)
	2. kammāḷaṉ (tivā.)
	3. necavāḷi. ``kammiyar kuḻīi'' (maturaik 521)
	4. poṟiviṉaiñaṉ, mechanic
kam - kammāḷaṉ (Pkt. kammara) = kollaṉ, taccaṉ, ciṟpaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, kaṉṉāṉ eṉṉum aivakaik kammiyaruḷ oru vakaiyaṉ.
kammāḷaṉ - kammāṇaṉ. ``kammāṇa cēriyum'' (S.I.I. ii). 43
o.nō. : kaḷavāḷi - kaḷavāṇi, vaḷari - vaṇari.
kollaṉaik kuṟikkum karumakaṉ eṉṉum col, karumam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirinta taṉiccol aṉṟu; irumpai yuṇarttum karu eṉṉum collum, āṭavaṉaik kuṟikkum makaṉ eṉṉum collum cērnta kūṭṭuc collākum.
karumakaṉ - karumāṉ - karumaṉ - kollaṉ (piṅ.)
karuṅkol = irumpu (cūṭā.)
karuṅkollaṉ = karumāṉ. karumakaṉ = kollaṉ. ``karumakak kammiyaṉ'' (kampa. pampā. 37) karumam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu karumakaṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟiyiruppiṉ, `karumak kammiyaṉ' eṉṟu mikaipaṭu collāṟ kuṟikka vēṇṭiyatillai.
iṉi, karumaiyeṉṉuñ coṟku valimaip poruḷum (puṟam. 2; cilap. 5, 29) iruppatāl, `karuṅkaik kollaṉ' (cilap. 5, 29) eṉṉum toṭaraic cāṉṟākak koṇṭu, karumakaṉ eṉpataṟku valiyavaṉ allatu vaṉ ceyalaṉ eṉṟu poruḷuraikkavum iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, aḵtu attuṇaic ciṟappum porutta muṭaiyataṉṟu.
karumāṉ eṉpatu, cemmāṉ (shoemaker) eṉpataṉoṭu niṟam paṟṟi muraṇpaṭṭatu. cemmakaṉ - cemmāṉ.
o.nō : blacksmith x white smith (worker in tin).
cemmāṉ eṉpatai, Carman (S) eṉṉum tōṟ peyariṉ tiripākac ceṉṉait tamiḻakarāti kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum.
cf. E.	tan. to convert raw hide into leather, to make brown by exposure to sun.
oru kulattaik kuṟikkumpōtu, palarpāṟ peyaroṭu akarac cāriyai cērttuk kuṟippatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam.
e-ṭu :	orumaip peyar	paṉmaippeyar	kulappeyar
	taṭṭāṉ	taṭṭār	taṭṭārakkulam
	cekkāṉ	cekkār	cekkārakkulam
	vaṇṇāṉ	vaṇṇār	vaṇṇārakkulam
immuṟaippaṭi, karumāṉ colliṉiṉṟu karumārak kulam eṉpatu amaiyum. itai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu kollaṉ peyar Karmara eṉṟu irukku vētattil amaintatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ceytalaik kuṟikkum karu eṉṉum viṉaimutaṉilai, vaṭamoḻi yil karu (kr) eṉṟum, ilattīṉil (cres) eṉṟum niṟkum. ōr iyalpāṉa vērccol uyir mutalāy allatu uyir meymmutalāy irukkumē yaṉṟi, meymmutalāy irātu. karma, karṇa, karttā, kāraṇa, kārya mutaliya tiricoṟkaḷellām, vaṭamoḻiyilum uyir meym mutalāyē yiruttal kāṇka.
12
etu tēvamoḻi?
ulakiluḷḷa (ēṟattāḻa) mūvāyira moḻikaḷuḷ, orucila, maṟai nūluṭaimai paṟṟit tammait tūyamoḻi (Holy Language) eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum camaṟkirutam taṉṉait tēvamoḻi (Divine Language) eṉṟum, pōṟṟip pukaḻntu koḷkiṉṟaṉa. tūyamoḻi eṉpatiṉum tēvamoḻi eṉpatu uyarvāṉatu. muṉṉatu maṇṇulakattil teyvat taṉmai yaṭainta makkaḷ moḻi yeṉṟum, piṉṉatu viṇṇulakatti ṉiṉṟō vīṭṭulakattiṉiṉṟō maṇṇulakattiṟku vanta tēvarmoḻi yeṉṟum, karuttup piṟappippaṉa.
ulakil oru moḻiyum tēvamoḻi yaṉṟu. oṉṟait tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, atu puṉainturaivakaiyākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam puṉainturai vakaiyiṟ kuṟittaṟkum, kīḻk kuṟikkappeṟum kuṇaṅkaḷaṉaittum ataṟkiruttal vēṇṭum.
(1)	ulakamoḻi mutaṉmai
(2)	oliyeḷimai
(3)	paṉmoḻit tāymai
(4)	oppuyarvaṟṟa paṇpāṭu
(5)	tūymai
(6)	maṟainūlum palkalai ilakkiyamum uṇmai
(7)	makkaṭ potuvurimai
(8)	naṭunilai aṟaṅkūṟal
iv veṇṇiyalpukaḷum oruṅkē tamiḻukkuḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭē camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷaṉa.
(1) vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa vēta āriya moḻiyum akkālattu vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷākiya (tamiḻ uṭpaṭṭa) pirākiruta moḻikaḷum kalanta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻiyē vaṭamoḻi yeṉṟu ciṟappāyk kūṟappaṭum camaṟkirutamām. (ki.mu. 2000).
tamiḻō, māntaṉ mutal mutal tōṉṟiya (Lemuria eṉṉum) kumarināṭṭil, tāṉē tōṉṟiya iyaṟkai moḻiyām (ki.mu. 50,000).
``ōṅka liṭaivantu......... taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ,'' eṉṉum paḻaiya taṉip pāvum itaṉaip pulappaṭuttum.
(2) ``āṟṟal aḻiyumeṉ ṟantaṇarkaḷ nāṉmaṟaiyaip
pōṟṟiyurait tēṭṭiṉ puṟatteḻutār - ēṭṭeḻuti
vallunarum vallārum vaḷḷuvaṉār muppālaic
colliṭiṉum āṟṟalcōr viṉṟu.''
eṉṟu kōtamaṉārum,
``ōtaṟ keḷitāy uṇartaṟ karitāki
............................................
vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻi māṇpu.''
eṉṟu māṅkuṭi marutaṉārum, kūṟiyavaiyē. tamiḻoli meṉmaikkum camaṟkirutavoli vaṉmaikkum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
(3) tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāyāyiruppatu pōṉṟē, camaṟkirutam vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāy eṉṟoru tavaṟāṉa karuttu neṭuṅkālamāka nilavi varukiṉṟatu. oru kālattilum ulaka vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkāta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiyak kalavaimoḻi, eṅṅaṉam tāymoḻiyāyiruttal kūṭum? oru moḻi mutaṟkaṇ taṉimoḻiyā yiruntālaṉṟō piṉṉart tāymoḻiyāyum amaital kūṭum! tamiḻ, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum, āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamumā yirukkumpōtu, av vāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oliyaḷavil mutuvaḷarcci yaṭainta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutam eṅṅaṉam uṇmaiyil paṉmoḻit tāyāyiruttal ollum?
(4) ``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar tarukkumāṟu, ilakkaṇac cemmaiyum vēṟem moḻiyiṉumillāta poruḷilakkaṇamumuṭaiya tamiḻē moḻip paṇpāṭṭil talaiciṟantatām.
(5) peruñ colvaḷa muṭaiyatum piṟamoḻit tuṇai vēṇṭātatum taṉittu vaḷarntōṅka vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē,
``aṉṟiyum tamiḻnūṟ kaḷavilai avaṟṟuḷ
x‹nw ahÆD« jÅ¤jÄ G©nlh?''
eṉṉum cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟu. moḻiyārāycci yillāta iruṭkālat teḻuntatātaliṉ, atu iṟṟaikkup poruḷaṟṟateṉa viṭukka.
camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷa colvaḷam paṉmoḻic cērkkaiyāl uṇṭāṉatātaliṉ, atuvum ciṟappilateṉṟu viṭukka.
(6) ``pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē''	(1336)
   ``maṟaimoḻi kiḷanta mantirat tāṉa''	(1421)
``niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa''	(1434)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyar kūṟṟum,
``ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam
tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampanīr nilamu lōkam,
māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa''
eṉṉum paḻantaṉippāṭalum, paṇṭait tamiḻil maṟainūlum palkalai ilakkiyamum iruntu piṉṉar iṟantupaṭṭamaiyai uṇarttum. paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyam iṟantupaṭṭamaiyiṉālēyē, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam iṉṟu mutal tōṉṟiya mūlamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
(7) āriya vētattaiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa camaṟkiruta nūl kaḷaiyum tamiḻarāṉa cūttirar ōtiṉ, avar nāvai aṟuttuviṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟum, avaṟṟaik kēṭpiṉ avar kātil urukkiṉa īyattai vārttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āriya aṟanūlkaḷ aṟaikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻaiyum tamiḻ nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟaṟkum kēṭṭaṟkumō ettakaiya kulamata kaṭciyiṉa vilakkum vēṟupāṭumillai.
``ceyyā moḻikkum tiruvaḷ ḷuvarmoḻinta
poyyā moḻikkum poruḷoṉṟē - ceyyā
ataṟkuriyar antaṇarē ārāyiṉ ēṉai
itaṟkuriyar allātār il''
eṉṟu veḷḷivītiyārum pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
(8) āriya aṟanūlkaḷ yāvum kaṭukaḷavum naṭunilaiyaṟiyātavai yeṉpatu, avai kulattiṟkoru muṟaiyum taṇṭamum kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu teḷivāy aṟiyappaṭum.
``vaḷḷuvarcey tirukkuṟaḷai maṟuvaṟanaṉ kuṇarntōrkaḷ
uḷḷuvarō maṉuvāti orukulattiṟ korunīti''
eṉṉum cuntarampiḷḷai vāyurai īṇṭuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ pallāṟṟāṉum talaiciṟanta moḻiyāyum, civaneṟiyum tirumālneṟiyum tūyatamiḻ mataṅkaḷāyum, iruppavum, tamiḻ potuvaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takāta moḻiyeṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭapiṉṉum, tamiḻar āriyariṉum tāḻntavareṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭapiṉṉum, tamiḻum tamiḻilakkiyamum iḻappiṟkum aḻippiṟkum iṭaṉāy, iṟṟaic ciṟumaiyaṭaintuḷḷaṉa. aṟivum ārāycciyum mikka ikkālattilēṉum, tamiḻar viḻitteḻuntu tam muṉṉōr nilaimaiyaṭaiya muyalvārāka.
- ``teṉmoḻi'' cepṭampar 1959
13
camaṟkirutavākkam - coṟkaḷ
vēta āriyar nāvalantēyam allatu nāvalampoḻil eṉṉum intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu, iṅkirunta ilakkiyam tamiḻilakkiyamē. avar intiya nākarikam tamateṉak kāṭṭit tammaik kumukāya (camutāya) nilaiyil ucca nilaikku uyarttuvāṉ vēṇṭi, tamiḻnūlkaḷai moḻi peyarttu ōr ilakkiyattai amaittaṟkup pōtiya colvaḷam tam muṉṉōr moḻikkiṉmaiyāl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷait tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭamainta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyē camaṟkirutam. itaṟku, ēṟkeṉavē iruvēṟu nilaikaḷil nērallā vaḻiyiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭ ṭirunta tamiḻccoṟkaḷum tuṇaiceytaṉa. itaṉāl, kīḻaiyāriyam mūvēṟu kālattil tamiḻccoṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ koṇṭamai aṟiyappaṭum.
avaṟṟuḷ:
mutaṟkālam mēlaiyāriyak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai mutaṟ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the First Period) eṉat takum.
iraṇṭāṅ kālam vēta āriyak kālamākum. akkālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai iraṇṭāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Second Period) eṉat takum.
mūṉṟāṅ kālam camaṟkirutak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai mūṉṟāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Third Period) eṉattakum.
1. mēlaiyāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
e-ṭu:
tiyūttāṉiyam:
amma - amma, āṉ ( = aṅku) - yon, iru - are, irum(pu) - iron, īṉ - yean, ean; uraṟu - roar, karai - cry, kāṇ - con, cun, can; kuraṅku   (= vaḷai) - crank, kuruḷ - curl, koṭukku - crook, cappu - sup, sip;       turuva - through, nāku - nag, paṭṭu (aṭi) - beat, pullam - bull, metu - smooth, muṉṉu - mun (think), vēṇṭu - want.
ilattīṉam
amar - amo (love), amor; uruḷai - uruṇṭa - rota (wheel), urai - or, kummal - cumulus, camam - similis, cēr - ser, tā - do, nāvāy - navis, paṭaku - barca, purai - par (equal), maṉ - man (remain), mutir - maturus, mel - mellow (soft), val - val (strong), vari (vaḷai) - vers (turn).
kirēkkam
aracaṉ - arthos, arkhon, kuru (niṟam) - chroma, kōṇam - gona, cāyai (niḻal) - skia, taccaṉ - tekton, tiruppu - trepos, turappaṇam - trapanon, tolai - telos, narampu - neuron, nīram - neros (wet), pala - polus, pari - peri (round), paṟaḻ - proles, pāḻi (nakaram) - polis, purai - poros, mattikai - mastix, mastigos, māl - melas (black), viḻai - philos.
potu
pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriyattil pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkup potuvākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
a. (cēymaic cuṭṭu) - E. a (that), Du. a (dat). Ger. a (das).
i. (aṇmaic cuṭṭu) - E. i (this) OE. (hit), Goth (hita), L. (hic).
ī. (aṇmaic cuṭṭu) - E. (these).
itōḷ (ivviṭam) - E. hither. OE. hider, L. citra.
(iy) - iyal - L. i, e; Gk. i ei; Goth. i. Slav. i, Lith. ei.
iyalutal = iyaṅkutal, naṭattal, cellutal. uy(iy) - iya - iyaṅku. iya - iyavu = celavu, vaḻi. iya - iyavuḷ = vaḻi. uytal = cellutal. uyttal = celuttutal.
ēr (kalappai). E ear., L. ar.
oṉ - oṉṉu, oṉṟu - E. one. Du. een, Ger. ein, L. unus, Gk. oinos.
kotti = pūṉai (kaṉṉaṭac col). pakaliṟ kuruṭu pōliruppatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. L. catta; E.cat.
cālai - Ger. saal, It. sala, salone, F. salon, E. saloon, E.hall Du. and Dan. hal.
pati - patam - pātam. nilattiṟ pativatu patam allatu pātam. pātam - pātai = vaḻi. patam = vaḻi. aṭittaṭam toṭarntu pativataṉāl oru vaḻi amaiyum. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippākam. atu, kāl, pātam, kīḻiṭam mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷai yuṇarttum. pātam eṉpatu nilattiṟ patiyakkūṭiya paranta aṭippakuti. pātam vaitta viḷakku eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. aṭimaram paruttatu, marattaṭiyil uṭkārntāṉ, mēṭaikkaṭiyiṟ pār, aṭināḷiliruntu collivarukiṉṟēṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷil pātam eṉṉum col poruntāmai kāṇka.
patam, pātam - Gk. podos. L. pedis. OE.fot (E.foot), Ger. fuss.
pātai - E. path, OE. paeth, Dut. pad, Ger. pfad.
bghW - OE., OHG. ber., Goth. bair, E.bear, L.fer, Gk. pher.
pōṭu - E.put, pot. pyt; Dan. putte. L. pon.
pōkaviṭu - pōkku - pōṭu.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷuḷ, kīḻaiyāriyattil illātaṉavu muḷa. avai mēlaiyāriyattiṉiṉṟu pirintu varumpōtu kīḻaiyāriyattāl iḻakkappaṭṭaṉavākum.
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriyattiṉum kīḻaiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu, piṉṉataṉ piṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
ammā - O.G. amma - Skt. amba.
kāṇ - OE. con - cna (metathesis) - E.know,
OHG. cna, L. and Gk. gno - Skt. Jna (Ph).
muḻuku - L. merg, Skt. majj.
irum(pu) - O.E.iren. O.G. Mod Ger. eise, Goth. eisarn.
	L. aer. aes; Skt. ayas.
val - L. val Skt. bal.
cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kīḻaiyāriyattiṉum mēlaiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu, vaṭatiraviṭam vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāyt tirintu airōppāvil muḻuvāriyamāy māṟiyataiyum, kīḻaiyāriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉ vaṭaintiya vaṭivattaic cila coṟkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭataiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:
umpar - va.ti. ūppar, Teut. upper, over; L. super, Gk. huper. Skt. upari.
āyiram. ayir - (ayiram) - aciram). North Indian. hazar, Persian. hazar Gk. kesloi; kilioi; Skt. sahasra (sa+hasra).
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ tiyūttāṉiyattiṉum ilattīṉil tirin tiruppatu, vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa tiraviṭam airōppāviṉ vaṭamēl     mūlaiyai muṭṭit tirumpiyataiyum, tirumpukālil kiḻakku nōkkit tirintataiyum kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
turuva - Teut. through, L. trans.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu - turuva (vi.e.).
turuttu - Teut. thrust, L. trud (o) (intrude, protrude, obtrude etc).
2. vētavāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
vētavāriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu (ki.mu.2000 - 1500), aṅku vāḻntavaruṭ perumpālār tiraviṭarē. peluccittāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum iṉṟum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataiyum, kāḷik kōṭṭam (Calcutta) eṉṉum peyar tūya tamiḻc collā yiruppataiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
āriya vētam vaṭatiraviṭam vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭil iyaṟṟap peṟṟamaiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ atiṟ pukuntuviṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: akavu, avai - cavai, ilakku, uttaram, uru - uruvu - uruvam, ulaku, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, kaṭukam, kālam, karuḷ (karumai), kumaraṉ - kumari, takkaṇam, taṇṭam, tūtu, naṭam, pakkam, maṇṭalam, mantiram, muttu - muttam, vaṭṭam.
virivañci ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkē iṅku mūlam viḷakkappeṟum.
amalutal = neruṅkutal. amartal = neruṅkutal. amaital = neruṅkutal, niṟaital, poruntutal, cērtal, kūṭutal. tiraḷutal.         amai = tiraṇṭa keṭṭi mūṅkil.
amai - avai = kūṭṭam. ma-va, pōli.
o.nō: ammai - avvai, kumi - kuvi.
avai - cavai. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷ collākkattil moḻi mutal meykaḷ ēṟappeṟum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cakaram.
e-ṭu :	uruḷ - curuḷ, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ - cēṇ, ēmam - cēmam, capā (Sabha) eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ poruttamāṉa vērillai.
uttaram = uyaram, uyaramāṉa vaṭaticai. takkaṇam = tāḻvu, tāḻvāṉa teṉticai, kumarimalai muḻukip paṉimalai tōṉṟiyapiṉ, nāvalattil vaṭaticai yuyarntu teṉṟicai tāḻntatu. takkut toṇṭai = tāḻnta kural. kuṭamalaiyāl uyarntiruppatu mēṟṟicaiyeṉṟum, kuṇakaṭalāl tāḻntiruppatu kīḻtticai yeṉṟum vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal. uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivuṭaip poruḷ. uru - uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumai vaṭivam, ataik kāṭṭum iṭaiccol. o.nō: aḷavu - aḷapu. uruvu - uruvam. tamiḻilakkaṇam āriya varukaikku eṇṇāyiramāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatu.
ulavutal = vaḷaital, cuṟṟutal. ulā = nakaraic cuṟṟi varum pavaṉi. ulakkai = uruṇṭa perumpiṭi. ulamvā - ulamā. ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ulavu - ulaku = uruṇṭaiyāyiruppatu allatu cuṟṟi varuvatu. o.nō: koṭkutal = cuṟṟi varutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ulaku - ulakam. lōk eṉṉum vaṭacol, pārttatu allatu pārkkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu nōkku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripām.
iṉi, ulakam vaṭṭamāyirukkum ñālam (pūmi) eṉṟumām. o.nō: ``kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam'' (kuṟun. 300).
ukaram uyarattaik kuṟikkum vērccol. e-ṭu: ukkam = talai, uvvi = talai, utti = oruvakait talaiyaṇi. ucci, umpar, uvaṇ, uyar.
uvaṇ = mēliṭam. uvaṇai = tēvarulakam. uvaṇam = uyarcci, paruntu, kaluḻaṉ, kaḻuku. paruntu uyarap paṟakkum paṟavaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ``cauîau¥ gwªjhY« C®¡FUÉ gUªjhFkh?'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. uvaṇam - cuvaṇam = paruntu, kaluḻaṉ. kaḻuku. uvaṇam (cuvaṇam) mullai nilattup paṟavaiyātalāl, an nilatteyvamākiya māyōṉukku ūrtiyākak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
cuvaṇam eṉṉum collai vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cuparṇa eṉṟu tirittup piṉpu cu + parṇa eṉṟu pirittu, nalla ilai, aḻakiya ilaiyuḷḷatu, aḻakiya ilai pōṉṟa ciṟakaiyuṭaiyatu, eṉṟu poruntāp puḷukalākak karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar. kaṭukaḷavēṉum pakuttaṟi vuṭaiyār, nakaiccuvaiyāṉa ic coṟporuṭ karaṇiyattaik kāṇiṉ nakātirār.
kummutal = tiraḷutal. kum - kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ. kumari = tiraṇṭavaḷ, iḷaiñai. o.nō: L. virginem, E. virgin from virgo to swell. viṭaittal = paruttal. vilaṅkiṉattilum paṟavai yiṉattilum tiraṇṭu iḷaimaiyā yiruppavaṟṟai viṭaiyeṉpatu marapu. āṭu, māṭu, kutirai, māṉ, pūṉai, kōḻi, pāmpu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, tiraṇṭu iḷamaiyāyiruppavaṟṟai viṭaiyeṉpatu iruvakai vaḻakkilumuṇmai kaṇṭukoḷka. viṭai - viṭalai = iḷaṅkāḷai, maṟavaṉ (vīraṉ), iḷaiñaṉ, 16 mutal 30 āṇṭu varaippaṭṭavaṉ, tiṇṇiyōṉ. iḷanīrai viṭalaiyeṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku. uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum āṇ paruttiruppatāl, viṭaiyeṉpatu iḷavāṇaiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. kumar eṉṉum col uyartiṇaiyil irupāṟ potuvām. kumari eṉpatu, iḷamaipaṟṟic cila marappeyarkkum, putumai (mutaṉmai) kuṟittuc cila toḻiṟpeyarkkum, aḻiyāmai kuṟittuc cila iṭappeyar poruṭ peyarkkum aṭaimoḻiyām.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷai, vaṭavar kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi, muṟaiyē tamiḻp poruḷoṭu makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉar.
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭai = muṭṭai. muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭai.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kālai maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam. vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaḷaittal.
maṇṭalam eṉpatu irukkuvētap perumpiriviṉ peyar. tamiḻc ceyyuḷil ellāvaṭikaḷum aḷavottuvarum yāppu maṇṭilam eṉappaṭum.
``ottā ḻicaiyum maṇṭila yāppum
kuṭṭamum nēraṭik koṭṭiṉa eṉpa''	(cey. 114)
``maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivai yiraṇṭum
centūk kiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar''	(cey. 116)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ. ``eṉpa'', ``eṉmaṉār pulavar'' eṉṉum vaḻinūṉ marapuraikaḷ, `maṇṭilam' eṉṉuñ col mutaṉūṟ kuṟiyī ṭeṉpatait terivikkum. mutaṉūl āriya varukaikku muṟpaṭṭateṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
maṇṭalam, maṇṭilam eṉṉum irucollum vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ kaḷaiyum, tōṟṟattaiyum celavaiyum kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum toṉṟutoṭṭu iruvakai vaḻakkilum kuṟittu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpatu allatu nāṟpattaintu nāṭ koṇṭatu maruttuva nūl kūṟum kāla maṇṭalam. toṇṭai maṇṭalam, koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa paḻaimai yāṉa iṭamaṇṭalam. ``maṇṭilat tarumaiyum'' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
taḷ - tāḷ = marañ ceṭi koṭi payir pulliṉ taṭitta aṭi; ilai pūviṉ aṭi tāḷai oṭṭiya payir pullilai; tāḷ pōṉṟa kāl, viḷakkuttaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa.
taḷ - taṇṭu = ceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi. (e-ṭu: kīraittaṇṭu), taṇṭu pōṉṟa poruḷ (e-ṭu: viḷakkuttaṇṭu).
taṇṭu - taṇṭāṉ = taṇṭukkōrai. taṇṭutal = taṇṭupōl tiraṭṭutal, paṇattait tiraṭṭutal.
taṇṭu = taṭi, paṭaku valikkuṅ kōl, taṇṭiṉāl tāṅkum pallakku, taṇṭupōl tiraṇṭa paṭai.
o.nō: club = stick association. staff = stick, body of officers.
taṇṭu - taṇṭaṉ = kōl.
taḷ - taṭṭu, taṭṭai = cōḷap payir pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi = parumaṉ mikuti, taṭi, vīṇaik kōṭu, taṭitta mūṅkil tāṅkum pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyam = tāṅkum kaṭṭai. taṇṭi - taṇṭikai = pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyal = taṇṭikai.
taṇṭittal = paruttal, taṭiyāl aṭittu oṟuttal, oṟuttal.
taṇṭi - taṭi. o.nō: piṇṭi - piṭi, kaṇṭi - kaṭi, maṇṭi - maṭi, 
aṇṭi - aṭi. itu tokuttal tiripu.
taṭittal = paruttal. taṭiyaṉ = paruttavaṉ, parutta pūcaṇikkāy.
taṭi - taṭippu - taṭippam. taṭi - taṭimpu - taṭimpal.
taṇṭu - taṇṭam = taṭi; paṭai. taṇṭattalaivaṉ = paṭaittalaivaṉ.
taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam (oṟuppu). taṇṭi + aṉam = taṇṭaṉam (oṟuppu). taṇṭi + aṉai = taṇṭaṉai (oṟuppu).
taṇṭā eṉpatu kalveṭṭuk kuṟikkum mūvakait taṇṭaṉaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
taḷ eṉṉum vērccoṟkē paruttaṟ poruḷuḷatu.
taḷ - taḷam = kaṉam, tiṇmai (cīvaka. 719, urai).
taḷam - taṭam = parumai. ``taṭavum kayavum naḷiyum perumai''. (tol. 803).
taṭā = parumai. (kallāṭam, 8 : 15), perumpāṉai (tivā.).
taṭavuttāḻi = peruñcāṭi.
taḷ - tāḷ - tāḷi = kūntaṟpaṉai. tāḷi = tāḻi = perumpāṉai. 
tāḷ - tāṭṭi = parumai, valimai.
`taḻaṅku' eṉṉum col `muḻaṅku' eṉpatupōl olip perumaiyai uṇarttum.
muṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal.
muṉṉam = maṉam (tivā.). muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam.
muṉ - maṉ. maṉ + tiram = (maṉṟiram) - mantiram.
o.nō: maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - mantai.
muṉṟu - muntu, piṉṟu - pintu.
mantiram = iṉṉatu ākaveṉṟu uṟutiyāy eṇṇutal, aṅṅaṉam eṇṇic collum urai.
``niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa''	(1434)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāvaiyum,
``eṇṇiya veṇṇiyāṅ keytupa veṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ''	(kuṟaḷ. 666)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷaiyum, tirumūlar tirumantirattaiyum mantiram collic cavittuviṭṭāṉ, mantirattiṉāl vāyaik kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷaiyum; mantiri, mantiram (cūḻviṉai) eṉṉum araciyaṟ coṟkaḷaiyum, kūrntu nōkkuka.
vāymoḻi eṉpatu mantirattiṉ maṟupeyar. nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalil nammāḻvār aruḷicceyta mutalāyiram tiruvāy moḻi yeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
ākavē, mantiram eṉṉum col tamiḻil araciyal, mataviyal, ilakkaṇam ākiya muttuṟaikkuriyatākum. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya varacukaḷ varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāt toṉmai vāyntavai. paṇṭaināḷiṟ kiḻakkattiya nāṭukaṭkut tamiḻnāṭṭoṭu araciyal toṭarpum vaṇikattoṭarpum iruntatiṉāl, mantiri eṉṉum col malēyā cīṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum paraviyirukkiṉṟatu. Mandarin eṉṉum āṅkilaccol pōrttukīciya vāyilāyp piṟkālattiṟ pukuntatākum. ataṟkumuṉ mēlaiyāriyattil ic col illai.
camaṟkiruta iru paṭaikaḷ
1. aṭippaṭai - vēta āriyam
2. mēṟpaṭai	1) vaṭa tiraviṭam (pirākirutam)
		2) teṉ tiraviṭam (tamiḻoḻinta pañca tiraviṭam)
		3) tamiḻ
pirākirutam eṉpatu. vētak kālattil vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷ. pirākirutam = muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu. camaṟkirutam eṉpatu, vētamoḻi yilakkaṇa vamaitiyaip perumpālum taḻuvi, am moḻiyoṭu pirākirutattaiyum tiraviṭattaiyum (teṉ tiraviṭam) tamiḻaiyum kalantu ceyta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭaimoḻi yākum. camaṟkirutam = naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
pirākirutam eṉṉum col pākatam eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum col caṅkatam, caṉukkirakam allatu cāṉukkirakam eṉṟum, tiriyum.
pirākirutam camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyateṉpatu ataṉ peyarālum moḻiyālum teḷivāyt teriyiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutam tirintatākap pētaimai mikka paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiyum ārāycciyillāta ayalāraiyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mayakki varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam), camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyāmalum pākatattil vaḻaṅkuvatē.
e-kā:	tiriyātavai		tirintavai
	caṣṭi (sashti)	caṭṭi (satihi)
	tarma (dharma)	tamma (dhamma)
tirintu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭāntam (truṣṭānta), cōttam (tōtra) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ pōṉṟavaiyē. avai yāvum aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷalla. pirākirutattiṟkum aṭippaṭai tamiḻē āyiṉum, atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟātavāṟu attuṇai vaṭa coṟkaḷ atiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟ kalappu tamiḻil iruppataiviṭat tiraviṭattil mikutiyākavum, tiraviṭattil iruppataiviṭap pākatattil mikutiyākavum, irukkum. vaṭakkē cellac cella vaṭacoṟ kalappu mikum eṉa aṟika. vēta āriya moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉataṉāl, atu paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷiṟ kalantu pulluruviyum uṇṇiyum pēṉum pōl mēluṇṇiyāy (Parasite) iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āriyac col vērūṉṟiyavuṭaṉ ataṟku nērāṉa teṉcol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōm. vaḻakkaṟṟa col ilakkiyattil iṭam peṟāviṭiṉ maṟaintu mīḷā nilaiyaṭaiyum.
pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭattiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ perumpālum īṟu tirintē vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu :	tamiḻ	pirākirutam	tamiḻ	pirākirutam
	accaṉ	ajja	kaṭṭai	kaṭṭa
	attai	attā	kampam	kampa
	ēḻakam	ēḷaka	nēyam	nēyam
	aiyaṉ	ayya	vaṭṭam	vaṭṭa

	2) putuccol eṉṟum;

	2) iṭukuṟi eṉṟum;
ivviru pālāyp pakukkappaṭum.
paḻañcol eṉpaṉa vēta āriyam. iṭukuṟikaḷ `āṉanta pairavi', `kaliyāṇi', `caṅkarāparaṇam', `taṉṉiyāci', `nāta nāmak kiriyai', mutaliya paṇṇup peyarkaḷ pōṉṟavai.

(2) vērccol :	e-ṭu :	paṇ - pāṇi (kai), maṉ - manu.

ēmāṟṟu vakaikaḷ
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum tiraviṭac coṟkaḷumāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyilirukkavum, avaṟṟaiyellām vaṭa colleṉak kāṭṭa vēṇṭip palvēṟu ēmāṟṟu muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar vaṭa nūlār.
